   52:         boxShadow: '0 0 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)',
   53:         flexShrink: 0,
   54:       }}
   55:     >
   56:       <rect width="30" height="20" fill="#DA251D" />
   57:       <polygon
   58:         points="15,4 16.5,8.8 21.5,8.8 17.5,11.8 19,16.5 15,13.5 11,16.5 12.5,11.8 8.5,8.8 13.5,8.8"
   59:         fill="#FFFF00"
   60:       />
   61:     </svg>
   62:   );
   63: };
   64: 
   65: interface LanguageOption {
   66:   key: LocaleType;
   67:   label: string;
   68:   flag: React.ReactNode;
   69: }
   70: 
   71: const LANGUAGES: LanguageOption[] = [
   72:   {
   73:     key: 'en',
   74:     label: 'English (US)',
   75:     flag: <UkFlag size={20} />,
   76:   },
   77:   {
   78:     key: 'vi',
   79:     label: 'Vietnamese',
   80:     flag: <VnFlag size={20} />,
   81:   },
   82: ];
   83: 
   84: const LanguageDropdown: React.FC = () => {
   85:   const { locale, setLocale } = useTranslation();
   86: 
   87:   const current = LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.key === locale) || LANGUAGES[1];
   88: 
   89:   const menuItems = LANGUAGES.map((item) => {
   90:     const isActive = item.key === locale;
   91:     return {
   92:       key: item.key,
   93:       label: (
   94:         <div
   95:           style={{
   96:             display: 'flex',
   97:             alignItems: 'center',
   98:             gap: 12,
   99:             padding: '8px 12px',
  100:             fontSize: 14,
  101:             fontWeight: isActive ? 600 : 400,
  102:             color: '#1e293b',
  103:             background: isActive ? '#e6f4ff' : 'transparent',
  104:             borderRadius: 6,
  105:             transition: 'all 0.15s ease',
  106:           }}
  107:         >
  108:           {item.flag}
  109:           <span>{item.label}</span>
  110:         </div>
  111:       ),
  112:       style: {
  113:         padding: 2,
  114:         backgroundColor: 'transparent',
  115:       },
  116:       onClick: () => {
  117:         setLocale(item.key);
  118:       },
  119:     };
  120:   });
  121: 
  122:   return (
  123:     <Dropdown
  124:       menu={{

  126:         style: {

  130:           border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
  131:           minWidth: 160,
  132:         },
  133:       }}
  134:       placement="bottomRight"
  135:       trigger={['click']}

  137:       <div
  138:         style={{
  139:           display: 'inline-flex',

  142:           cursor: 'pointer',
  143:           padding: '6px 10px',

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/layout/NotificationDropdown.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Badge, Button, Dropdown, Empty, List, Tabs, Tag, Tooltip, Typography } from 'antd';
    3: import { BellOutlined, CheckOutlined, DeleteOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    5: 
    6: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    7: import { useWebSocket } from '@/hooks/useWebSocket';
    8: import { notificationApi } from '@/services/api/notification';
    9: import type { NotificationResponse } from '@/services/types/notification';
   10: 
   11: const { Text } = Typography;
   12: 
   13: const PRIORITY_COLOR: Record<string, string> = {
   14:   LOW: 'default',
   15:   NORMAL: 'blue',
   16:   HIGH: 'orange',
   17:   URGENT: 'red',
   18: };
   19: 
   20: const NotificationDropdown = () => {
   21:   const { user } = useAuth();
   22:   const { subscribe } = useWebSocket();
   23: 
   24:   const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);
   25:   const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<NotificationResponse[]>([]);
   26:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   27:   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
   28:   const [tab, setTab] = useState<'all' | 'unread'>('all');
   29:   const mountedRef = useRef(true);
   30: 
   31:   // ═══ Load unread count ═══
   32:   const fetchUnreadCount = useCallback(async () => {
   33:     try {
   34:       const count = await notificationApi.getUnreadCount();
   35:       if (mountedRef.current) setUnreadCount(count);
   36:     } catch {
   37:       /* silent */
   38:     }
   39:   }, []);
   40: 
   41:   // ═══ Load inbox ═══
   42:   const fetchInbox = useCallback(async () => {
   43:     setLoading(true);
   44:     try {
   45:       const res = await notificationApi.getMyInbox({
   46:         page: 0,
   47:         size: 20,
   48:         isRead: tab === 'unread' ? false : undefined,
   49:       });
   50:       if (mountedRef.current) setNotifications(res.content);
   51:     } catch (err) {
   52:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   53:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không tải được thông báo');
   54:     } finally {
   55:       if (mountedRef.current) setLoading(false);
   56:     }
   57:   }, [tab]);
   58: 
   59:   // ═══ Init + subscribe WS ═══
   60:   useEffect(() => {
   61:     mountedRef.current = true;
   62:     fetchUnreadCount();
   63:     return () => {
   64:       mountedRef.current = false;
   65:     };
   66:   }, [fetchUnreadCount]);
   67: 
   68:   useEffect(() => {
   69:     if (open) fetchInbox();
   70:   }, [open, tab, fetchInbox]);
   71: 
   72:   useEffect(() => {
   73:     if (!user?.accountId) return;
   74: 
   75:     const unsub = subscribe(
   76:       `/topic/user/${user.accountId}/notifications`,
   77:       (payload: any) => {
   78:         // Message có thể là notification mới HOẶC sự kiện read/mark
   79:         if (payload?.action === 'MARKED_READ') {
   80:           setNotifications((prev) =>
   81:             prev.map((n) => (n.notificationId === payload.notificationId ? { ...n, isRead: true } : n))
   82:           );
   83:           setUnreadCount((c) => Math.max(0, c - 1));
   84:           return;
   85:         }
   86:         if (payload?.action === 'MARKED_ALL_READ') {
   87:           setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, isRead: true })));
   88:           setUnreadCount(0);
   89:           return;
   90:         }
   91: 
   92:         // Notification mới
   93:         const newNotif: NotificationResponse = {
   94:           id: payload.id ?? payload.notificationId ?? 0,
   95:           notificationId: payload.notificationId ?? 0,
   96:           senderAccountId: payload.senderAccountId,
   97:           senderName: payload.senderName,
   98:           notificationType: payload.notificationType ?? 'SYSTEM',
   99:           title: payload.title ?? '',
  100:           content: payload.content,
  101:           referenceType: payload.referenceType,
  102:           referenceId: payload.referenceId,
  103:           extraData: payload.extraData,
  104:           priority: payload.priority ?? 'NORMAL',
  105:           createdAt: payload.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
  106:           isRead: false,
  107:         };
  108: 
  109:         setNotifications((prev) => [newNotif, ...prev]);
  110:         setUnreadCount((c) => c + 1);
  111:         toast.success(`🔔 ${newNotif.title}`, { duration: 4000 });
  112:       }
  113:     );
  114: 
  115:     return () => unsub();
  116:   }, [subscribe, user?.accountId]);
  117: 
  118:   // ═══ Actions ═══
  119:   const handleMarkRead = async (n: NotificationResponse) => {
  120:     if (n.isRead) return;
  121:     try {
  122:       await notificationApi.markAsRead(n.notificationId);
  123:       setNotifications((prev) =>
  124:         prev.map((x) => (x.notificationId === n.notificationId ? { ...x, isRead: true } : x))
  125:       );
  126:       setUnreadCount((c) => Math.max(0, c - 1));
  127:     } catch (err) {
  128:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  129:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Thao tác thất bại');
  130:     }
  131:   };
  132: 
  133:   const handleMarkAll = async () => {
  134:     try {
  135:       await notificationApi.markAllAsRead();
  136:       setNotifications((prev) => prev.map((n) => ({ ...n, isRead: true })));
  137:       setUnreadCount(0);
  138:       toast.success('Đã đánh dấu tất cả đã đọc');
  139:     } catch (err) {
  140:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  141:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Thao tác thất bại');
  142:     }
  143:   };
  144: 
  145:   const handleDelete = async (e: React.MouseEvent, n: NotificationResponse) => {
  146:     e.stopPropagation();
  147:     try {
  148:       await notificationApi.delete(n.notificationId);
  149:       setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((x) => x.notificationId !== n.notificationId));
  150:       if (!n.isRead) setUnreadCount((c) => Math.max(0, c - 1));
  151:       toast.success('Đã xóa thông báo');
  152:     } catch (err) {
  153:       const e2 = err as { message?: string };
  154:       toast.error(e2?.message || 'Xóa thất bại');
  155:     }
  156:   };
  157: 
  158:   const formatRelative = (iso?: string) => {
  159:     if (!iso) return '';
  160:     const diff = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
  161:     const m = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
  162:     if (m < 1) return 'vừa xong';
  163:     if (m < 60) return `${m} phút trước`;
  164:     const h = Math.floor(m / 60);
  165:     if (h < 24) return `${h} giờ trước`;
  166:     const d = Math.floor(h / 24);
  167:     return `${d} ngày trước`;
  168:   };
  169: 
  170:   // ═══ Content ═══
  171:   const content = (
  172:     <div
  173:       style={{
  174:         width: 380,
  175:         background: '#fff',
  176:         borderRadius: 12,
  177:         boxShadow: '0 8px 24px -4px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.15), 0 2px 6px -1px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.08)',
  178:         border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
  179:         overflow: 'hidden',
  180:       }}
  181:     >
  182:       <div
  183:         style={{
  184:           padding: '14px 16px',
  185:           borderBottom: '1px solid #f1f5f9',
  186:           display: 'flex',
  187:           justifyContent: 'space-between',
  188:           alignItems: 'center',
  189:           background: '#fafbff',
  190:         }}
  191:       >
  192:         <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 15 }}>
  193:           Thông báo
  194:         </Text>
  195:         <Button
  196:           type="link"
  197:           size="small"
  198:           icon={<CheckOutlined />}
  199:           onClick={handleMarkAll}
  200:           disabled={unreadCount === 0}
  201:           style={{ fontSize: 12 }}
  202:         >
  203:           Đọc tất cả
  204:         </Button>
  205:       </div>
  206: 
  207:       <Tabs
  208:         activeKey={tab}
  209:         onChange={(k) => setTab(k as 'all' | 'unread')}
  210:         size="small"
  211:         style={{ padding: '0 8px' }}
  212:         items={[
  213:           { key: 'all', label: 'Tất cả' },
  214:           { key: 'unread', label: `Chưa đọc${unreadCount ? ` (${unreadCount})` : ''}` },
  215:         ]}
  216:       />
  217: 
  218:       <div style={{ maxHeight: 420, overflowY: 'auto' }}>
  219:         {loading ? (
  220:           <div style={{ padding: 32, textAlign: 'center', color: '#94a3b8' }}>Đang tải...</div>
  221:         ) : notifications.length === 0 ? (
  222:           <Empty
  223:             image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE}
  224:             description="Không có thông báo"
  225:             style={{ padding: 32 }}
  226:           />
  227:         ) : (
  228:           <List
  229:             dataSource={notifications}
  230:             renderItem={(item) => (
  231:               <List.Item
  232:                 onClick={() => handleMarkRead(item)}
  233:                 style={{
  234:                   padding: '12px 16px',
  235:                   cursor: 'pointer',
  236:                   background: item.isRead ? '#fff' : '#f0f7ff',
  237:                   borderBottom: '1px solid #f8fafc',
  238:                   transition: 'background 0.15s',
  239:                 }}
  240:                 actions={[
  241:                   <Tooltip key="del" title="Xóa">
  242:                     <Button
  243:                       type="text"
  244:                       size="small"
  245:                       danger
  246:                       icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
  247:                       onClick={(e) => handleDelete(e, item)}
  248:                     />
  249:                   </Tooltip>,
  250:                 ]}
  251:               >
  252:                 <List.Item.Meta
  253:                   title={
  254:                     <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
  255:                       {!item.isRead && (
  256:                         <span
  257:                           style={{
  258:                             width: 8,
  259:                             height: 8,
  260:                             borderRadius: '50%',
  261:                             background: '#1677ff',
  262:                             display: 'inline-block',
  263:                           }}
  264:                         />
  265:                       )}
  266:                       <Text strong={!item.isRead} style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
  267:                         {item.title}
  268:                       </Text>
  269:                       <Tag
  270:                         color={PRIORITY_COLOR[item.priority] || 'default'}
  271:                         style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 10, lineHeight: '16px' }}
  272:                       >
  273:                         {item.priority}
  274:                       </Tag>
  275:                     </div>
  276:                   }
  277:                   description={
  278:                     <div style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>
  279:                       {item.content && (
  280:                         <Text
  281:                           type="secondary"
  282:                           style={{ fontSize: 12, display: 'block' }}
  283:                           ellipsis
  284:                         >
  285:                           {item.content}
  286:                         </Text>
  287:                       )}
  288:                       <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
  289:                         {formatRelative(item.createdAt)}
  290:                       </Text>
  291:                     </div>
  292:                   }
  293:                 />
  294:               </List.Item>
  295:             )}
  296:           />
  297:         )}
  298:       </div>
  299:     </div>
  300:   );
  301: 
  302:   return (
  303:     <Dropdown
  304:       dropdownRender={() => content}
  305:       trigger={['click']}
  306:       open={open}
  307:       onOpenChange={setOpen}
  308:       placement="bottomRight"
  309:     >
  310:       <Badge count={unreadCount} size="small" overflowCount={99} offset={[-2, 2]}>
  311:         <Button
  312:           type="text"
  313:           icon={<BellOutlined style={{ fontSize: 18 }} />}
  314:           style={{ width: 40, height: 40 }}
  315:         />
  316:       </Badge>
  317:     </Dropdown>
  318:   );
  319: };
  320: 
  321: export default NotificationDropdown;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Layout, Menu } from 'antd';
    2: import type { MenuProps } from 'antd';
    3: import {
    4:   DashboardOutlined,
    5:   BookOutlined,
    6:   TeamOutlined,
    7:   FileTextOutlined,
    8:   SettingOutlined,
    9:   UserOutlined,
   10:   ProfileOutlined,
   11:   UserAddOutlined,
   12: } from '@ant-design/icons';
   13: import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
   14: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
   15: import { ADMIN_MENU, INSTRUCTOR_MENU } from '@/constants/routes';
   16: import type { MenuItem } from '@/constants/routes';
   17: import { useTranslation } from '@/hooks/useTranslation';
   18: 
   19: const { Sider } = Layout;
   20: 
   21: type AntdMenuItem = NonNullable<MenuProps['items']>[number];
   22: 
   23: const ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {
   24:   DashboardOutlined: <DashboardOutlined />,
   25:   BookOutlined: <BookOutlined />,
   26:   TeamOutlined: <TeamOutlined />,
   27:   FileTextOutlined: <FileTextOutlined />,
   28:   SettingOutlined: <SettingOutlined />,
   29:   UserOutlined: <UserOutlined />,
   30:   ProfileOutlined: <ProfileOutlined />,
   31:   UserAddOutlined: <UserAddOutlined />,
   32: };
   33: 
   34: const MENU_KEY_TRANSLATION_MAP: Record<string, string> = {
   35:   '/admin/dashboard': 'menu.dashboard',
   36:   'leads': 'menu.leads',
   37:   '/admin/leads': 'menu.leadList',
   38:   'students': 'menu.students',
   39:   '/admin/students': 'menu.studentList',
   40:   'courses': 'menu.courses',
   41:   '/admin/courses': 'menu.courseList',
   42:   'users': 'menu.users',
   43:   '/admin/users': 'menu.userList',
   44:   'system': 'menu.system',
   45:   '/admin/roles': 'menu.roles',
   46:   '/admin/audit-logs': 'menu.auditLogs',
   47:   'reports': 'menu.reports',
   48:   '/admin/reports': 'menu.generalReports',
   49:   '/instructor/dashboard': 'menu.dashboard',
   50:   '/instructor/courses': 'menu.myCourses',
   51:   '/instructor/students': 'menu.students',
   52:   '/instructor/assignments': 'menu.assignments',
   53:   '/instructor/profile': 'common.profile',
   54: };
   55: 
   56: interface Props {
   57:   collapsed: boolean;
   58: }
   59: 
   60: const Sidebar = ({ collapsed }: Props) => {
   61:   const { isAdmin } = useAuth();
   62:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   63:   const location = useLocation();
   64:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   65: 
   66:   const renderItem = (item: MenuItem): AntdMenuItem => ({
   67:     key: item.key,
   68:     label: MENU_KEY_TRANSLATION_MAP[item.key] ? t(MENU_KEY_TRANSLATION_MAP[item.key]) : item.label,
   69:     icon: item.icon ? ICON_MAP[item.icon as string] : undefined,
   70:     children: item.children?.map(renderItem),
   71:   });
   72: 
   73:   const menu = isAdmin() ? ADMIN_MENU : INSTRUCTOR_MENU;
   74:   const items = menu.map(renderItem);
   75: 
   76:   // Tìm key match với path hiện tại
   77:   const getSelectedKey = (): string => {
   78:     const path = location.pathname;
   79:     for (const item of menu) {
   80:       if (item.path && path === item.path) return item.key;
   81:       if (item.children) {
   82:         for (const child of item.children) {
   83:           if (child.path && path === child.path) return child.key;
   84:         }
   85:       }
   86:     }
   87:     return path;
   88:   };
   89: 
   90:   return (
   91:     <Sider
   92:       trigger={null}
   93:       collapsible
   94:       collapsed={collapsed}
   95:       width={240}
   96:       style={{
   97:         overflow: 'auto',
   98:         height: '100vh',
   99:         position: 'sticky',
  100:         top: 0,
  101:         left: 0,
  102:       }}
  103:     >
  104:       <div
  105:         style={{
  106:           height: 64,
  107:           display: 'flex',
  108:           alignItems: 'center',
  109:           justifyContent: 'center',
  110:           color: '#fff',
  111:           fontSize: collapsed ? 16 : 18,
  112:           fontWeight: 700,
  113:           letterSpacing: 1,
  114:           borderBottom: '1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.1)',
  115:         }}
  116:       >
  117:         {collapsed ? 'E' : 'ECMS'}
  118:       </div>
  119: 
  120:       <Menu
  121:         theme="dark"
  122:         mode="inline"
  123:         selectedKeys={[getSelectedKey()]}
  124:         defaultOpenKeys={menu.filter((m) => m.children).map((m) => m.key)}
  125:         items={items}
  126:         onClick={({ key }) => {
  127:           if (typeof key === 'string' && key.startsWith('/')) {
  128:             navigate(key);
  129:           }
  130:         }}
  131:       />
  132:     </Sider>
  133:   );
  134: };
  135: 
  136: export default Sidebar;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/components/layout/UserDropdown.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { Dropdown, Avatar, Space } from 'antd';
    2: import {
    3:   UserOutlined,
    4:   LogoutOutlined,
    5: } from '@ant-design/icons';
    6: import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    7: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    8: import { useAuth } from '@/hooks/useAuth';
    9: import { useTranslation } from '@/hooks/useTranslation';
   10: import { ROLE_LABEL } from '@/constants/roles';
   11: 
   12: const UserDropdown = () => {
   13:   const { user, logout, isAdmin } = useAuth();
   14:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   15:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   16: 
   17:   const handleLogout = async () => {
   18:     try {
   19:       await logout();
   20:       toast.success(t('common.logout'));
   21:       navigate('/login', { replace: true });
   22:     } catch {
   23:       toast.error('Logout failed');
   24:     }
   25:   };
   26: 
   27:   const profilePath = isAdmin() ? '/admin/profile' : '/instructor/profile';
   28: 
   29:   const menuItems = [
   30:     {
   31:       key: 'profile',
   32:       icon: <UserOutlined />,
   33:       label: t('common.profile'),
   34:       onClick: () => navigate(profilePath),
   35:     },
   36:     { type: 'divider' as const },
   37:     {
   38:       key: 'logout',
   39:       icon: <LogoutOutlined />,
   40:       label: t('common.logout'),
   41:       danger: true,
   42:       onClick: handleLogout,
   43:     },
   44:   ];
   45: 
   46:   return (
   47:     <Dropdown menu={{ items: menuItems }} placement="bottomRight" trigger={['click']}>
   48:       <Space style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>
   49:         <Avatar
   50:           size={32}
   51:           src={user?.avatarUrl || undefined}
   52:           icon={!user?.avatarUrl ? <UserOutlined /> : undefined}
   53:           style={{ backgroundColor: '#1677ff' }}
   54:         />
   55:         <div style={{ lineHeight: 1.2 }}>
   56:           <div style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500 }}>{user?.fullName || 'User'}</div>
   57:           <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#8c8c8c' }}>
   58:             {user?.roleCode ? ROLE_LABEL[user.roleCode] : ''}
   59:           </div>
   60:         </div>
   61:       </Space>
   62:     </Dropdown>
   63:   );
   64: };
   65: 
   66: export default UserDropdown;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/useAuth.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
    2: 
    3: /**
    4:  * Hook truy cập auth state.
    5:  *
    6:  * @example
    7:  * const { user, isAuthenticated, login, logout } = useAuth();
    8:  */
    9: export const useAuth = () => {
   10:   const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user);
   11:   const isAuthenticated = useAuthStore((s) => s.isAuthenticated);
   12:   const isLoading = useAuthStore((s) => s.isLoading);
   13:   const initialized = useAuthStore((s) => s.initialized);
   14: 
   15:   const login = useAuthStore((s) => s.login);
   16:   const logout = useAuthStore((s) => s.logout);
   17:   const fetchMe = useAuthStore((s) => s.fetchMe);
   18:   const setUser = useAuthStore((s) => s.setUser);
   19:   const clear = useAuthStore((s) => s.clear);
   20: 
   21:   const hasRole = useAuthStore((s) => s.hasRole);
   22:   const isAdmin = useAuthStore((s) => s.isAdmin);
   23:   const isInstructor = useAuthStore((s) => s.isInstructor);
   24:   const isStudent = useAuthStore((s) => s.isStudent);
   25: 
   26:   return {
   27:     user,
   28:     isAuthenticated,
   29:     isLoading,
   30:     initialized,
   31:     login,
   32:     logout,
   33:     fetchMe,
   34:     setUser,
   35:     clear,
   36:     hasRole,
   37:     isAdmin,
   38:     isInstructor,
   39:     isStudent,
   40:   };
   41: };

    1: // src/hooks/useChat.ts
    2: import { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
    3: import { useAuth } from './useAuth';
    4: import { connectWs, subscribeWs } from '../services/wsClient';
    5: import * as chatService from '../services/api/chat';
    6: import type {
    7:   Conversation,
    8:   Message,
    9:   MessagePermission,
   10:   Participant,
   11:   ParticipantRole,
   12:   WsErrorPayload,
   13:   WsMessageDeletedEvent,
   14:   WsNameChangedEvent,
   15:   WsParticipantChangedEvent,
   16:   WsPermissionChangedEvent,
   17:   WsReadEvent,
   18: } from '../services/types/chat';
   19: 
   20: /** Số tin nhắn mỗi lần tải (khớp default của BE) */
   21: const MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
   22: /** Số hội thoại tải về (BE cap 100) */
   23: const CONVERSATION_PAGE_SIZE = 100;
   24: 
   25: type AnyErr = { response?: { data?: { message?: string } }; message?: string };
   26: 
   27: /** Payload của /topic/user/{accountId}/conversations */
   28: type UserConversationEvent = {
   29:   action?: 'NEW_CONVERSATION' | 'REMOVED_FROM_CONVERSATION' | 'PREFERENCE_CHANGED';
   30:   conversationId?: number;
   31:   /** BE gửi "pinned" | "mutedByUser" | "archived" */
   32:   field?: string;
   33:   value?: boolean;
   34: };
   35: 
   36: /**
   37:  * PREFERENCE_CHANGED của BE gửi field dạng "pinned" | "mutedByUser" | "archived"
   38:  * trong khi response HTTP dùng isPinned / isMutedByUser / isArchived.
   39:  */
   40: const applyPreference = (
   41:   conv: Conversation,
   42:   field: unknown,
   43:   value?: boolean,
   44: ): Conversation => {
   45:   const v = Boolean(value);
   46:   if (field === 'pinned' || field === 'isPinned') return { ...conv, isPinned: v };
   47:   if (field === 'mutedByUser' || field === 'isMutedByUser') return { ...conv, isMutedByUser: v };
   48:   if (field === 'archived' || field === 'isArchived') return { ...conv, isArchived: v };
   49:   return conv;
   50: };
   51: 
   52: export const useChat = (onError?: (msg: string) => void) => {
   53:   const { user } = useAuth();
   54:   const currentUserId = user?.accountId;
   55: 
   56:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   57:   // ERROR — giữ callback trong ref để mọi handler giữ identity ổn định
   58:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   59:   const onErrorRef = useRef(onError);
   60:   useEffect(() => {
   61:     onErrorRef.current = onError;
   62:   }, [onError]);
   63: 
   64:   const notify = useCallback((msg: string) => {
   65:     onErrorRef.current?.(msg);
   66:   }, []);
   67: 
   68:   const errMsg = useCallback(
   69:     (err: unknown, fallback: string) => {
   70:       const e = err as AnyErr | undefined;
   71:       notify(e?.response?.data?.message || e?.message || fallback);
   72:     },
   73:     [notify],
   74:   );
   75: 
   76:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   77:   // STATE
   78:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   79:   const [conversations, setConversations] = useState<Conversation[]>([]);
   80:   const [activeConversation, setActiveConversation] = useState<Conversation | null>(null);
   81:   const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);
   82:   const [participants, setParticipants] = useState<Participant[]>([]);
   83:   const [typingUsers, setTypingUsers] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
   84:   /** accountId → lastReadMessageId (từ topic .../read) */
   85:   const [readReceipts, setReadReceipts] = useState<Record<number, number | null>>({});
   86:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   87:   const [loadingMessages, setLoadingMessages] = useState(false);
   88:   const [loadingMore, setLoadingMore] = useState(false);
   89:   const [hasMoreMessages, setHasMoreMessages] = useState(false);
   90: 
   91:   /** page kế tiếp dùng để tải tin nhắn cũ hơn */
   92:   const olderPageRef = useRef(1);
   93: 
   94:   const totalUnread = useMemo(
   95:     () => conversations.reduce((s, c) => s + (c.unreadCount || 0), 0),
   96:     [conversations],
   97:   );
   98: 
   99:   const currentParticipant = useMemo(
  100:     () => participants.find((p) => p.accountId === currentUserId) ?? null,
  101:     [participants, currentUserId],
  102:   );
  103: 
  104:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  105:   // FETCH
  106:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  107:   const fetchConversations = useCallback(async () => {
  108:     setLoading(true);
  109:     try {
  110:       const list = await chatService.getMyConversations(0, CONVERSATION_PAGE_SIZE);
  111:       setConversations(Array.isArray(list) ? list : []);
  112:     } catch (err) {
  113:       errMsg(err, 'Không tải được danh sách hội thoại');
  114:     } finally {
  115:       setLoading(false);
  116:     }
  117:   }, [errMsg]);
  118: 
  119:   const fetchParticipants = useCallback(async (convId: number) => {
  120:     try {
  121:       const parts = await chatService.getParticipants(convId);
  122:       setParticipants(Array.isArray(parts) ? parts : []);
  123:     } catch (err) {
  124:       console.error('[useChat] fetchParticipants:', err);
  125:     }
  126:   }, []);
  127: 
  128:   const fetchMessages = useCallback(
  129:     async (convId: number) => {
  130:       setLoadingMessages(true);
  131:       try {
  132:         const res = await chatService.getMessages(convId, 0, MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE);
  133:         setMessages(res);
  134:         olderPageRef.current = 1;
  135:         setHasMoreMessages(res.length >= MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE);
  136:         if (res.length > 0) {
  137:           const lastId = res[res.length - 1].id;
  138:           chatService.markAsRead(convId, lastId).catch(() => {});
  139:           setConversations((prev) =>
  140:             prev.map((c) => (c.id === convId ? { ...c, unreadCount: 0 } : c)),
  141:           );
  142:         }
  143:       } catch (err) {
  144:         errMsg(err, 'Không tải được tin nhắn');
  145:       } finally {
  146:         setLoadingMessages(false);
  147:       }
  148:     },
  149:     [errMsg],
  150:   );
  151: 
  152:   /**
  153:    * BE trả page 0 = MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE tin MỚI NHẤT (đã sort tăng dần),
  154:    * page 1 = lô cũ hơn kế tiếp → tải thêm bằng cách tăng page.
  155:    */
  156:   const loadOlderMessages = useCallback(async () => {
  157:     if (!activeConversation || loadingMore || !hasMoreMessages) return;
  158:     setLoadingMore(true);
  159:     try {
  160:       const page = olderPageRef.current;
  161:       const older = await chatService.getMessages(activeConversation.id, page, MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE);
  162: 
  163:       if (older.length === 0) {
  164:         setHasMoreMessages(false);
  165:         return;
  166:       }
  167: 
  168:       setMessages((prev) => {
  169:         const ids = new Set(prev.map((m) => m.id));
  170:         const merged = older.filter((m) => !ids.has(m.id));
  171:         return [...merged, ...prev];
  172:       });
  173:       olderPageRef.current = page + 1;
  174:       setHasMoreMessages(older.length >= MESSAGE_PAGE_SIZE);
  175:     } catch (err) {
  176:       errMsg(err, 'Không tải thêm được tin nhắn');
  177:     } finally {
  178:       setLoadingMore(false);
  179:     }
  180:   }, [activeConversation, loadingMore, hasMoreMessages, errMsg]);
  181: 
  182:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  183:   // NAV
  184:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  185:   const selectConversation = useCallback(
  186:     async (conv: Conversation) => {
  187:       setActiveConversation(conv);
  188:       setMessages([]);
  189:       setParticipants([]);
  190:       setReadReceipts({});
  191:       setTypingUsers(new Set());
  192:       setHasMoreMessages(false);
  193:       olderPageRef.current = 1;
  194:       await Promise.all([fetchMessages(conv.id), fetchParticipants(conv.id)]);
  195:     },
  196:     [fetchMessages, fetchParticipants],
  197:   );
  198: 
  199:   const closeConversation = useCallback(() => {
  200:     setActiveConversation(null);
  201:     setMessages([]);
  202:     setParticipants([]);
  203:     setReadReceipts({});
  204:     setTypingUsers(new Set());
  205:     setHasMoreMessages(false);
  206:   }, []);
  207: 
  208:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  209:   // SEND
  210:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  211:   const sendMessage = useCallback(
  212:     async (content: string, replyToMessageId?: number | null) => {
  213:       const cid = activeConversation?.id;
  214:       const text = content.trim();
  215:       if (!cid || !text) return;
  216: 
  217:       try {
  218:         const created = await chatService.sendMessage(cid, {
  219:           messageType: 'TEXT',
  220:           content: text,
  221:           replyToMessageId: replyToMessageId || undefined,
  222:         });
  223: 
  224:         // WS echo có thể về trước khi HTTP resolve → dedupe theo id
  225:         if (created?.id) {
  226:           setMessages((prev) =>
  227:             prev.some((m) => m.id === created.id) ? prev : [...prev, created],
  228:           );
  229:         }
  230: 
  231:         setConversations((prev) =>
  232:           prev.map((c) =>
  233:             c.id === cid
  234:               ? {
  235:                   ...c,
  236:                   lastMessageId: created?.id ?? c.lastMessageId,
  237:                   lastMessageAt: created?.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
  238:                   lastMessagePreview: text.slice(0, 255),
  239:                   unreadCount: 0,
  240:                 }
  241:               : c,
  242:           ),
  243:         );
  244: 
  245:         return created;
  246:       } catch (err) {
  247:         errMsg(err, 'Gửi tin nhắn thất bại');
  248:         throw err;
  249:       }
  250:     },
  251:     [activeConversation?.id, errMsg],
  252:   );
  253: 
  254:   /** BE ECMS chưa có endpoint typing → no-op (giữ API cho UI) */
  255:   const sendTyping = useCallback(() => {}, []);
  256: 
  257:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  258:   // WS — kết nối 1 lần (wsClient tự dedupe)
  259:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  260:   useEffect(() => {
  261:     if (!currentUserId) return;
  262:     connectWs();
  263:   }, [currentUserId]);
  264: 
  265:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  266:   // WS GLOBAL — errors + sự kiện cấp user
  267:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  268:   useEffect(() => {
  269:     if (!currentUserId) return;
  270: 
  271:     const unsubError = subscribeWs('/user/queue/errors', (payload: WsErrorPayload) => {
  272:       notify(payload?.message || 'Đã có lỗi xảy ra');
  273:     });
  274: 
  275:     const unsubUserConv = subscribeWs(
  276:       `/topic/user/${currentUserId}/conversations`,
  277:       (p: UserConversationEvent) => {
  278:         if (p?.action === 'NEW_CONVERSATION') {
  279:           fetchConversations();
  280:         } else if (p?.action === 'REMOVED_FROM_CONVERSATION') {
  281:           setConversations((prev) => prev.filter((c) => c.id !== p.conversationId));
  282:           setActiveConversation((prev) => {
  283:             if (prev?.id !== p.conversationId) return prev;
  284:             return null;
  285:           });
  286:           if (activeConversation?.id === p.conversationId) closeConversation();
  287:         } else if (p?.action === 'PREFERENCE_CHANGED') {
  288:           setConversations((prev) =>
  289:             prev.map((c) =>
  290:               c.id === p.conversationId ? applyPreference(c, p.field, p.value) : c,
  291:             ),
  292:           );
  293:         }
  294:       },
  295:     );
  296: 
  297:     return () => {
  298:       unsubError();
  299:       unsubUserConv();
  300:     };
  301:   }, [currentUserId, notify, fetchConversations, activeConversation?.id, closeConversation]);
  302: 
  303:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  304:   // WS CONVERSATION-SCOPED — 9 topics
  305:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  306:   useEffect(() => {
  307:     const cid = activeConversation?.id;
  308:     if (!cid) return;
  309: 
  310:     const unsubs: Array<() => void> = [];
  311: 
  312:     // 1. Tin nhắn mới
  313:     unsubs.push(
  314:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}`, (m: Message) => {
  315:         if (!m?.id) return;
  316:         setMessages((prev) => (prev.some((x) => x.id === m.id) ? prev : [...prev, m]));
  317:         if (m.senderId !== currentUserId) {
  318:           chatService.markAsRead(cid, m.id).catch(() => {});
  319:         }
  320:         setConversations((prev) =>
  321:           prev.map((c) =>
  322:             c.id === cid
  323:               ? {
  324:                   ...c,
  325:                   lastMessageId: m.id,
  326:                   lastMessageAt: m.createdAt,
  327:                   lastMessagePreview:
  328:                     m.messageType === 'TEXT'
  329:                       ? (m.content || '').slice(0, 255)
  330:                       : `[${m.messageType}]`,
  331:                   unreadCount: m.senderId === currentUserId ? 0 : c.unreadCount,
  332:                 }
  333:               : c,
  334:           ),
  335:         );
  336:       }),
  337:     );
  338: 
  339:     // 2. Đổi tên nhóm
  340:     unsubs.push(
  341:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}/name-changed`, (p: WsNameChangedEvent) => {
  342:         if (!p?.newName) return;
  343:         setActiveConversation((prev) =>
  344:           prev && prev.id === cid ? { ...prev, name: p.newName } : prev,
  345:         );
  346:         setConversations((prev) =>
  347:           prev.map((c) => (c.id === cid ? { ...c, name: p.newName } : c)),
  348:         );
  349:       }),
  350:     );
  351: 
  352:     // 3. Đổi quyền gửi tin
  353:     unsubs.push(
  354:       subscribeWs(
  355:         `/topic/conversation/${cid}/permission-changed`,
  356:         (p: WsPermissionChangedEvent) => {
  357:           if (!p?.messagePermission) return;
  358:           setActiveConversation((prev) =>
  359:             prev && prev.id === cid
  360:               ? { ...prev, messagePermission: p.messagePermission }
  361:               : prev,
  362:           );
  363:         },
  364:       ),
  365:     );
  366: 
  367:     // 4. Thành viên thay đổi (mute/ban/unban/remove)
  368:     unsubs.push(
  369:       subscribeWs(
  370:         `/topic/conversation/${cid}/participant-changed`,
  371:         (p: WsParticipantChangedEvent) => {
  372:           fetchParticipants(cid);
  373:           if (p?.action === 'REMOVED' && p.accountId === currentUserId) {
  374:             closeConversation();
  375:             fetchConversations();
  376:           }
  377:         },
  378:       ),
  379:     );
  380: 
  381:     // 5. Đổi vai trò
  382:     unsubs.push(
  383:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}/role-changed`, () => fetchParticipants(cid)),
  384:     );
  385: 
  386:     // 6. Chuyển quyền OWNER
  387:     unsubs.push(
  388:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}/ownership-transferred`, () =>
  389:         fetchParticipants(cid),
  390:       ),
  391:     );
  392: 
  393:     // 7. Đã xem
  394:     unsubs.push(
  395:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}/read`, (p: WsReadEvent) => {
  396:         if (p?.accountId == null) return;
  397:         setReadReceipts((prev) => ({ ...prev, [p.accountId]: p.lastReadMessageId ?? null }));
  398:       }),
  399:     );
  400: 
  401:     // 8. Sửa tin nhắn
  402:     unsubs.push(
  403:       subscribeWs(`/topic/conversation/${cid}/edit`, (updated: Message) => {
  404:         if (!updated?.id) return;
  405:         setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === updated.id ? updated : m)));
  406:       }),
  407:     );
  408: 
  409:     // 9. Xoá tin nhắn
  410:     unsubs.push(
  411:       subscribeWs(
  412:         `/topic/conversation/${cid}/message-deleted`,
  413:         (p: WsMessageDeletedEvent) => {
  414:           if (!p?.messageId) return;
  415:           setMessages((prev) =>
  416:             prev.map((m) => (m.id === p.messageId ? { ...m, isDeleted: true } : m)),
  417:           );
  418:         },
  419:       ),
  420:     );
  421: 
  422:     return () => {
  423:       unsubs.forEach((u) => {
  424:         try {
  425:           u();
  426:         } catch {
  427:           /* ignore */
  428:         }
  429:       });
  430:     };
  431:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  432:   }, [activeConversation?.id, currentUserId]);
  433: 
  434:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  435:   // HANDLERS — thành viên / quyền
  436:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  437:   const wrap = useCallback(
  438:     async (fn: () => Promise<unknown>, errText?: string) => {
  439:       const cid = activeConversation?.id;
  440:       try {
  441:         await fn();
  442:         if (cid) await fetchParticipants(cid);
  443:       } catch (err) {
  444:         errMsg(err, errText || 'Thao tác thất bại');
  445:         throw err;
  446:       }
  447:     },
  448:     [activeConversation?.id, fetchParticipants, errMsg],
  449:   );
  450: 
  451:   const handleGrantRole = useCallback(
  452:     (accountId: number, role: ParticipantRole) =>
  453:       wrap(
  454:         () => chatService.updateParticipantRole(activeConversation!.id, accountId, role),
  455:         'Không thể cấp quyền',
  456:       ),
  457:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  458:   );
  459: 
  460:   const handleRevokeRole = useCallback(
  461:     (accountId: number) =>
  462:       wrap(
  463:         () => chatService.updateParticipantRole(activeConversation!.id, accountId, 'MEMBER'),
  464:         'Không thể thu hồi quyền',
  465:       ),
  466:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  467:   );
  468: 
  469:   const handleMuteMember = useCallback(
  470:     (accountId: number, mutedUntil: string | null, reason?: string) =>
  471:       wrap(
  472:         () => chatService.muteParticipant(activeConversation!.id, accountId, mutedUntil, reason),
  473:         'Không thể mute thành viên',
  474:       ),
  475:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  476:   );
  477: 
  478:   const handleUnmuteMember = useCallback(
  479:     (accountId: number) =>
  480:       wrap(
  481:         () => chatService.unmuteParticipant(activeConversation!.id, accountId),
  482:         'Không thể bỏ mute',
  483:       ),
  484:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  485:   );
  486: 
  487:   const handleBanMember = useCallback(
  488:     (accountId: number, reason?: string) =>
  489:       wrap(
  490:         () => chatService.banParticipant(activeConversation!.id, accountId, reason),
  491:         'Không thể ban thành viên',
  492:       ),
  493:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  494:   );
  495: 
  496:   const handleUnbanMember = useCallback(
  497:     (accountId: number) =>
  498:       wrap(
  499:         () => chatService.unbanParticipant(activeConversation!.id, accountId),
  500:         'Không thể bỏ ban',
  501:       ),
  502:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  503:   );
  504: 
  505:   const handleTransferOwnership = useCallback(
  506:     (accountId: number) =>
  507:       wrap(
  508:         () => chatService.transferOwnership(activeConversation!.id, accountId),
  509:         'Không thể chuyển quyền OWNER',
  510:       ),
  511:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  512:   );
  513: 
  514:   const handleRemoveMember = useCallback(
  515:     (accountId: number) =>
  516:       wrap(
  517:         () => chatService.removeParticipant(activeConversation!.id, accountId),
  518:         'Không thể xoá thành viên',
  519:       ),
  520:     [activeConversation, wrap],
  521:   );
  522: 
  523:   const handleAddParticipants = useCallback(
  524:     async (accountIds: number[]) => {
  525:       const cid = activeConversation?.id;
  526:       if (!cid || accountIds.length === 0) return;
  527:       try {
  528:         const updated = await chatService.addParticipants(cid, accountIds);
  529:         if (Array.isArray(updated) && updated.length > 0) setParticipants(updated);
  530:         else await fetchParticipants(cid);
  531:       } catch (err) {
  532:         errMsg(err, 'Thêm thành viên thất bại');
  533:         throw err;
  534:       }
  535:     },
  536:     [activeConversation?.id, fetchParticipants, errMsg],
  537:   );
  538: 
  539:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  540:   // HANDLERS — hội thoại
  541:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  542:   const handleUpdateMessagePermission = useCallback(
  543:     async (perm: MessagePermission) => {
  544:       if (!activeConversation) return;
  545:       try {
  546:         await chatService.updateMessagePermission(activeConversation.id, perm);
  547:         setActiveConversation((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, messagePermission: perm } : prev));
  548:       } catch (err) {
  549:         errMsg(err, 'Không thể đổi quyền gửi tin');
  550:         throw err;
  551:       }
  552:     },
  553:     [activeConversation, errMsg],
  554:   );
  555: 
  556:   const handleUpdateName = useCallback(
  557:     async (newName: string) => {
  558:       if (!activeConversation) return;
  559:       const name = newName.trim();
  560:       if (!name) return;
  561:       try {
  562:         await chatService.updateConversationName(activeConversation.id, name);
  563:         setActiveConversation((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, name } : prev));
  564:         setConversations((prev) =>
  565:           prev.map((c) => (c.id === activeConversation.id ? { ...c, name } : c)),
  566:         );
  567:       } catch (err) {
  568:         errMsg(err, 'Không thể đổi tên nhóm');
  569:         throw err;
  570:       }
  571:     },
  572:     [activeConversation, errMsg],
  573:   );
  574: 
  575:   const createGroupConversation = useCallback(
  576:     async (name: string, participantIds: number[]) => {
  577:       try {
  578:         const conv = await chatService.createConversation({
  579:           conversationType: 'GROUP',
  580:           name: name.trim(),
  581:           participantIds,
  582:         });
  583:         await fetchConversations();
  584:         await selectConversation(conv);
  585:         return conv;
  586:       } catch (err) {
  587:         errMsg(err, 'Không thể tạo nhóm chat');
  588:         throw err;
  589:       }
  590:     },
  591:     [fetchConversations, selectConversation, errMsg],
  592:   );
  593: 
  594:   const startPrivateConversation = useCallback(
  595:     async (accountId: number) => {
  596:       try {
  597:         const conv = await chatService.createPrivateConversation(accountId);
  598:         await fetchConversations();
  599:         await selectConversation(conv);
  600:         return conv;
  601:       } catch (err) {
  602:         errMsg(err, 'Không thể bắt đầu trò chuyện');
  603:         throw err;
  604:       }
  605:     },
  606:     [fetchConversations, selectConversation, errMsg],
  607:   );
  608: 
  609:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  610:   // HANDLERS — tin nhắn
  611:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  612:   const deleteMessage = useCallback(
  613:     async (messageId: number) => {
  614:       try {
  615:         await chatService.deleteMessage(messageId);
  616:         setMessages((prev) =>
  617:           prev.map((m) => (m.id === messageId ? { ...m, isDeleted: true } : m)),
  618:         );
  619:       } catch (err) {
  620:         errMsg(err, 'Xoá tin nhắn thất bại');
  621:         throw err;
  622:       }
  623:     },
  624:     [errMsg],
  625:   );
  626: 
  627:   const editMessage = useCallback(
  628:     async (messageId: number, content: string) => {
  629:       try {
  630:         const updated = await chatService.editMessage(messageId, content);
  631:         setMessages((prev) => prev.map((m) => (m.id === messageId ? updated : m)));
  632:         return updated;
  633:       } catch (err) {
  634:         errMsg(err, 'Sửa tin nhắn thất bại');
  635:         throw err;
  636:       }
  637:     },
  638:     [errMsg],
  639:   );
  640: 
  641:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  642:   // HANDLERS — tuỳ chọn riêng của user
  643:   // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  644:   const handlePinConversation = useCallback(
  645:     async (convId: number, pinned: boolean) => {
  646:       try {
  647:         await chatService.pinConversation(convId, pinned);
  648:         setConversations((prev) =>
  649:           prev.map((c) => (c.id === convId ? { ...c, isPinned: pinned } : c)),
  650:         );
  651:       } catch (err) {
  652:         errMsg(err, 'Thao tác thất bại');
  653:         throw err;
  654:       }
  655:     },
  656:     [errMsg],
  657:   );
  658: 
  659:   const handleMuteConversationByUser = useCallback(
  660:     async (convId: number, muted: boolean) => {
  661:       try {
  662:         await chatService.muteConversationByUser(convId, muted);
  663:         setConversations((prev) =>
  664:           prev.map((c) => (c.id === convId ? { ...c, isMutedByUser: muted } : c)),
  665:         );
  666:       } catch (err) {
  667:         errMsg(err, 'Thao tác thất bại');
  668:         throw err;
  669:       }
  670:     },
  671:     [errMsg],
  672:   );
  673: 
  674:   const handleArchiveConversation = useCallback(
  675:     async (convId: number, archived: boolean) => {
  676:       try {
  677:         await chatService.archiveConversation(convId, archived);
  678:         setConversations((prev) =>
  679:           prev.map((c) => (c.id === convId ? { ...c, isArchived: archived } : c)),
  680:         );
  681:       } catch (err) {
  682:         errMsg(err, 'Thao tác thất bại');
  683:         throw err;
  684:       }
  685:     },
  686:     [errMsg],
  687:   );
  688: 
  689:   const getCurrentUserRole = useCallback((): ParticipantRole | null => {
  690:     const me = participants.find((p) => p.accountId === currentUserId);
  691:     return me?.roleInConversation ?? null;
  692:   }, [participants, currentUserId]);
  693: 
  694:   return {
  695:     // state
  696:     conversations,
  697:     activeConversation,
  698:     messages,
  699:     participants,
  700:     currentParticipant,
  701:     typingUsers,
  702:     readReceipts,
  703:     loading,
  704:     loadingMessages,
  705:     loadingMore,
  706:     hasMoreMessages,
  707:     totalUnread,
  708:     currentUserId,
  709:     // nav
  710:     selectConversation,
  711:     closeConversation,
  712:     fetchConversations,
  713:     fetchMessages,
  714:     fetchParticipants,
  715:     loadOlderMessages,
  716:     // messages
  717:     sendMessage,
  718:     sendTyping,
  719:     editMessage,
  720:     deleteMessage,
  721:     // conversation
  722:     createGroupConversation,
  723:     startPrivateConversation,
  724:     handleUpdateName,
  725:     handleUpdateMessagePermission,
  726:     handleAddParticipants,
  727:     handleRemoveMember,
  728:     // members
  729:     handleGrantRole,
  730:     handleRevokeRole,
  731:     handleMuteMember,
  732:     handleUnmuteMember,
  733:     handleBanMember,
  734:     handleUnbanMember,
  735:     handleTransferOwnership,
  736:     // preferences
  737:     handlePinConversation,
  738:     handleMuteConversationByUser,
  739:     handleArchiveConversation,
  740:     // helpers
  741:     getCurrentUserRole,
  742:   };
  743: };
  744: 
  745: export default useChat;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/useDebounce.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: 
    3: export const useDebounce = <T,>(value: T, delay = 500): T => {
    4:   const [debounced, setDebounced] = useState(value);
    5: 
    6:   useEffect(() => {
    7:     const timer = setTimeout(() => setDebounced(value), delay);
    8:     return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    9:   }, [value, delay]);
   10: 
   11:   return debounced;
   12: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/usePagination.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
    2: 
    3: interface UsePaginationReturn {
    4:   page: number;
    5:   pageSize: number;
    6:   setPage: (p: number) => void;
    7:   setPageSize: (s: number) => void;
    8:   reset: () => void;
    9: }
   10: 
   11: export const usePagination = (initialPage = 0, initialSize = 20): UsePaginationReturn => {
   12:   const [page, setPage] = useState(initialPage);
   13:   const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(initialSize);
   14: 
   15:   const reset = useCallback(() => {
   16:     setPage(initialPage);
   17:   }, [initialPage]);
   18: 
   19:   return { page, pageSize, setPage, setPageSize, reset };
   20: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/usePermission.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { useAuth } from './useAuth';
    2: import { hasRole, hasAnyRole, isAdmin, isInstructor } from '@/utils/permission';
    3: 
    4: export const usePermission = () => {
    5:   const { user } = useAuth();
    6: 
    7:   return {
    8:     hasRole: (role: string) => hasRole(user, role),
    9:     hasAnyRole: (roles: string[]) => hasAnyRole(user, roles),
   10:     isAdmin: () => isAdmin(user),
   11:     isInstructor: () => isInstructor(user),
   12:   };
   13: };

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/useTranslation.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { useCallback } from 'react';
    2: import { useLocaleStore } from '@/stores/localeStore';
    3: import { translate, type TranslationKey } from '@/locales';
    4: 
    5: export const useTranslation = () => {
    6:   const { locale, setLocale } = useLocaleStore();
    7: 
    8:   const t = useCallback(
    9:     (key: TranslationKey | string, defaultText?: string) => {
   10:       return translate(locale, key, defaultText);
   11:     },
   12:     [locale]
   13:   );
   14: 
   15:   return {
   16:     t,
   17:     locale,
   18:     setLocale,
   19:     isVi: locale === 'vi',
   20:     isEn: locale === 'en',
   21:   };
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export default useTranslation;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/hooks/useWebSocket.ts
// ============================================================
    1: // src/hooks/useWebSocket.ts
    2: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    3: import { useAuth } from './useAuth';
    4: import {
    5:   connectWs,
    6:   disconnectWs,
    7:   subscribeWs,
    8:   onWsStatusChange,
    9: } from '@/services/wsClient';
   10: 
   11: export const useWebSocket = () => {
   12:   const { user, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();
   13:   const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
   14: 
   15:   useEffect(() => {
   16:     if (!isAuthenticated || !user?.accountId) {
   17:       disconnectWs();
   18:       return;
   19:     }
   20:     connectWs();
   21:   }, [isAuthenticated, user?.accountId]);
   22: 
   23:   useEffect(() => {
   24:     return onWsStatusChange(setConnected);
   25:   }, []);
   26: 
   27:   return {
   28:     connected,
   29:     subscribe: subscribeWs,
   30:   };
   31: };
   32: 
   33: export default useWebSocket;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/locales/index.ts
// ============================================================
    1: import { vi } from './vi';
    2: import { en } from './en';
    3: import type { LocaleType } from '@/stores/localeStore';
    4: 
    5: export const dictionaries = {
    6:   vi,
    7:   en,
    8: };
    9: 
   10: export type TranslationKey = keyof typeof vi;
   11: 
   12: export const translate = (locale: LocaleType, key: TranslationKey | string, defaultText?: string): string => {
   13:   const dict = dictionaries[locale] || dictionaries.vi;
   14:   const val = (dict as Record<string, string>)[key];
   15:   if (val) return val;
   16:   return defaultText || key;
   17: };
   18:

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/admin/courses/CourseDetailPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
    3: import { Tabs } from 'antd';
    4: 
    5: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
    6: import PageLoading from '@/components/feedback/PageLoading';
    7: import ErrorState from '@/components/feedback/ErrorState';
    8: 
    9: import CourseDetailHeader from '@/components/course/CourseDetailHeader';
   10: import CourseOverviewCard from '@/components/course/CourseOverviewCard';
   11: import CourseResourceTree from '@/components/course/CourseResourceTree';
   12: import CourseSessionList from '@/components/course/CourseSessionList';
   13: import CourseQuestionList from '@/components/course/CourseQuestionList';
   14: 
   15: import { courseApi } from '@/services/api/courses';
   16: import type { CourseDetailResponse } from '@/services/types/course';
   17: 
   18: const CourseDetailPage = () => {
   19:   const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
   20:   const courseId = Number(id);
   21: 
   22:   const [course, setCourse] = useState<CourseDetailResponse | null>(null);
   23:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   24:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   25: 
   26:   const fetchCourse = async () => {
   27:     if (!courseId || isNaN(courseId)) {
   28:       setError('ID khóa học không hợp lệ');
   29:       setLoading(false);
   30:       return;
   31:     }
   32:     setLoading(true);
   33:     setError(null);
   34:     try {
   35:       const data = await courseApi.getById(courseId);
   36:       setCourse(data);
   37:     } catch (err) {
   38:       setError((err as { message?: string })?.message || 'Không tải được khóa học');
   39:     } finally {
   40:       setLoading(false);
   41:     }
   42:   };
   43: 
   44:   useEffect(() => {
   45:     fetchCourse();
   46:     // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
   47:   }, [courseId]);
   48: 
   49:   if (loading) return <PageLoading tip="Đang tải khóa học..." />;
   50:   if (error) return <ErrorState title="Lỗi" description={error} onRetry={fetchCourse} />;
   51:   if (!course) return <ErrorState description="Không tìm thấy khóa học" />;
   52: 
   53:   return (
   54:     <PageContainer>
   55:       <CourseDetailHeader course={course} onRefresh={fetchCourse} />
   56: 
   57:       <Tabs
   58:         defaultActiveKey="overview"
   59:         items={[
   60:           {
   61:             key: 'overview',
   62:             label: 'Tổng quan',
   63:             children: <CourseOverviewCard course={course} />,
   64:           },
   65:           {
   66:             key: 'resources',
   67:             label: `Nội dung (${course.resources?.length || 0})`,
   68:             children: <CourseResourceTree resources={course.resources || []} />,
   69:           },
   70:           {
   71:             key: 'sessions',
   72:             label: `Buổi học (${course.sessions?.length || 0})`,
   73:             children: <CourseSessionList sessions={course.sessions || []} />,
   74:           },
   75:           {
   76:             key: 'questions',
   77:             label: 'Câu hỏi',
   78:             children: <CourseQuestionList courseId={courseId} />,
   79:           },
   80:         ]}
   81:       />
   82:     </PageContainer>
   83:   );
   84: };
   85: 
   86: export default CourseDetailPage;

   25: const { Text } = Typography;

   27: const CourseListPage = () => {

   29:   const navigate = useNavigate();

   31:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   32:   // STATE
   33:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   34:   const [data, setData] = useState<CourseResponse[]>([]);
   35:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   36:   const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
   37:   const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
   38:   const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(20);
   39:   const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');
   40:   const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string | undefined>();
   41:   const [typeFilter, setTypeFilter] = useState<string | undefined>();
   42: 
   43:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   44:   // FETCH
   45:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   46:   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
   47:     setLoading(true);
   48:     try {
   49:       const params: Record<string, unknown> = {
   50:         page,
   51:         size: pageSize,
   52:         sortBy: 'createdAt',
   53:         sortDir: 'desc',
   54:       };
   55:       if (keyword.trim()) params.search = keyword.trim();
   56:       if (statusFilter) params.status = statusFilter;
   57:       if (typeFilter) params.type = typeFilter;
   58: 
   59:       const res = await courseApi.search(params);
   60:       setData(res.content);
   61:       setTotal(res.totalElements);
   62:     } catch (err) {
   63:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   64:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách khóa học');
   65:       setData([]);
   66:       setTotal(0);
   67:     } finally {
   68:       setLoading(false);
   69:     }
   70:   }, [page, pageSize, keyword, statusFilter, typeFilter]);
   71: 
   72:   useEffect(() => {
   73:     fetchData();
   74:   }, [fetchData]);
   75: 
   76:   // Reset page khi filter thay đổi
   77:   useEffect(() => {
   78:     setPage(0);
   79:   }, [keyword, statusFilter, typeFilter]);
   80: 
   81:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   82:   // HANDLERS
   83:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   84:   const handleRefresh = () => {
   85:     fetchData();
   86:     toast.success('Đã làm mới dữ liệu');
   87:   };
   88: 
   89:   const handleDelete = async (id: number) => {
   90:     try {
   91:       await courseApi.softDelete(id);
   92:       toast.success('Đã xóa khóa học');
   93:       fetchData();
   94:     } catch (err) {
   95:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   96:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Xóa thất bại');
   97:     }
   98:   };
   99: 
  100: 
  101:

    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { Card, Col, Row, Statistic, List, Tag, Typography, Spin, Empty } from 'antd';
    3: import {

   10: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';

   15: import { adminUserApi, auditLogApi } from '@/services/api/admin';

   17: import type { AuditLogResponse } from '@/services/types/admin';
   18: 
   19: const { Text } = Typography;
   20: 
   21: interface Stats {
   22:   totalUsers: number;
   23:   totalAdmins: number;
   24:   totalInstructors: number;
   25:   totalStudents: number;
   26:   totalCourses: number;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: const AdminDashboardPage = () => {
   30:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   31:   const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats | null>(null);
   32:   const [logs, setLogs] = useState<AuditLogResponse[]>([]);
   33:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   34: 
   35:   const fetchStats = async () => {
   36:     setLoading(true);
   37:     try {
   38:       // Parallel fetch — dùng size: 1 để lấy totalElements nhanh
   39:       const [allUsers, admins, instructors, students, courses, logPage] =
   40:         await Promise.all([
   41:           adminUserApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1 }),
   42:           adminUserApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, roleCode: 'ADMIN' }),
   43:           adminUserApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, roleCode: 'INSTRUCTOR' }),
   44:           adminUserApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, roleCode: 'STUDENT' }),
   45:           courseApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1 }),
   46:           auditLogApi.search({ page: 0, size: 10 }).catch(() => ({
   47:             content: [],
   48:             totalElements: 0,
   49:           }) as never),
   50:         ]);
   51: 
   52:       setStats({
   53:         totalUsers: allUsers.totalElements,
   54:         totalAdmins: admins.totalElements,
   55:         totalInstructors: instructors.totalElements,
   56:         totalStudents: students.totalElements,
   57:         totalCourses: courses.totalElements,
   58:       });
   59: 
   60:       setLogs(logPage.content || []);
   61:     } catch (err) {
   62:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   63:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải dữ liệu dashboard');
   64:     } finally {
   65:       setLoading(false);
   66:     }
   67:   };
   68: 
   69:   useEffect(() => {
   70:     fetchStats();
   71:   }, []);
   72: 
   73:   const handleRefresh = () => {
   74:     toast.success('Đang làm mới...');
   75:     fetchStats();
   76:   };
   77: 
   78:   return (
   79:     <PageContainer>
   80:       <PageHeader
   81:         title={t('dashboard.title')}
   82:         subtitle={t('dashboard.subtitle')}
   83:         extra={
   84:           <Button icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={handleRefresh}>
   85:             {t('common.refresh')}
   86:           </Button>
   87:         }
   88:       />
   89: 
   90:       {loading && !stats ? (
   91:         <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 80 }}>
   92:           <Spin size="large" />
   93:         </div>
   94:       ) : (
   95:         <>
   96:           {/* ── Stat Cards ── */}
   97:           <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
   98:             <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
   99:               <Card>
  100:                 <Statistic
  101:                   title={t('dashboard.totalUsers')}
  102:                   value={stats?.totalUsers ?? 0}
  103:                   prefix={<TeamOutlined />}
  104:                   valueStyle={{ color: '#1677ff' }}
  105:                 />
  106:               </Card>
  107:             </Col>
  108:             <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
  109:               <Card>
  110:                 <Statistic
  111:                   title={t('dashboard.totalInstructors')}
  112:                   value={stats?.totalInstructors ?? 0}
  113:                   prefix={<SolutionOutlined />}
  114:                   valueStyle={{ color: '#52c41a' }}
  115:                 />
  116:               </Card>
  117:             </Col>
  118:             <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
  119:               <Card>
  120:                 <Statistic
  121:                   title={t('dashboard.totalStudents')}
  122:                   value={stats?.totalStudents ?? 0}
  123:                   prefix={<UserOutlined />}
  124:                   valueStyle={{ color: '#faad14' }}
  125:                 />
  126:               </Card>
  127:             </Col>
  128:             <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
  129:               <Card>
  130:                 <Statistic
  131:                   title={t('dashboard.totalCourses')}
  132:                   value={stats?.totalCourses ?? 0}
  133:                   prefix={<BookOutlined />}
  134:                   valueStyle={{ color: '#eb2f96' }}
  135:                 />
  136:               </Card>
  137:             </Col>
  138:           </Row>
  139: 
  140:           {/* ── Chi tiết + Audit log ── */}
  141:           <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
  142:             <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
  143:               <Card title={t('dashboard.totalUsers')} style={{ height: '100%' }}>
  144:                 <Row gutter={16}>
  145:                   <Col span={12}>
  146:                     <Statistic
  147:                       title={t('dashboard.totalAdmins')}
  148:                       value={stats?.totalAdmins ?? 0}
  149:                     />
  150:                   </Col>
  151:                   <Col span={12}>
  152:                     <Statistic
  153:                       title={t('dashboard.totalInstructors')}
  154:                       value={stats?.totalInstructors ?? 0}
  155:                     />
  156:                   </Col>
  157:                 </Row>
  158:                 <Row gutter={16} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
  159:                   <Col span={12}>
  160:                     <Statistic
  161:                       title={t('dashboard.totalStudents')}
  162:                       value={stats?.totalStudents ?? 0}
  163:                     />
  164:                   </Col>
  165:                   <Col span={12}>
  166:                     <Statistic
  167:                       title={t('common.all')}
  168:                       value={(stats?.totalUsers ?? 0)
  169:                         - (stats?.totalAdmins ?? 0)
  170:                         - (stats?.totalInstructors ?? 0)
  171:                         - (stats?.totalStudents ?? 0)}
  172:                     />
  173:                   </Col>
  174:                 </Row>
  175:               </Card>
  176:             </Col>
  177: 
  178:             <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
  179:               <Card
  180:                 title={
  181:                   <span>
  182:                     <AuditOutlined /> {t('dashboard.recentActivity')}
  183:                   </span>
  184:                 }
  185:                 style={{ height: '100%' }}
  186:                 bodyStyle={{ maxHeight: 320, overflow: 'auto' }}
  187:               >
  188:                 {logs.length === 0 ? (
  189:                   <Empty description={t('dashboard.noActivity')} />
  190:                 ) : (
  191:                   <List
  192:                     size="small"
  193:                     dataSource={logs}
  194:                     renderItem={(log) => (
  195:                       <List.Item>
  196:                         <List.Item.Meta
  197:                           title={
  198:                             <Text style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
  199:                               <Tag color="blue">{log.action}</Tag>
  200:                               <Text type="secondary"> {log.entityType}</Text>
  201:                               {log.entityId ? ` #${log.entityId}` : ''}
  202:                             </Text>
  203:                           }
  204:                           description={
  205:                             <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
  206:                               Actor #{log.actorId} ·{' '}
  207:                               <DateDisplay value={log.createdAt} format="relative" />
  208:                             </Text>
  209:                           }
  210:                         />
  211:                       </List.Item>
  212:                     )}
  213:                   />
  214:                 )}
  215:               </Card>
  216:             </Col>
  217:           </Row>
  218:         </>
  219:       )}
  220:     </PageContainer>
  221:   );
  222: };
