   30:   CommentOutlined,
   31:   CheckCircleOutlined,
   32:   CloseCircleOutlined,
   33:   FormOutlined,
   34:   TeamOutlined,
   35:   ClockCircleOutlined,
   36:   HourglassOutlined,
   37:   PhoneOutlined,
   38:   MailOutlined,
   39:   TrophyOutlined,
   40:   BookOutlined,
   41:   UserOutlined,
   42:   ClearOutlined,
   43:   MessageOutlined,
   44:   CalendarOutlined,
   45:   FileTextOutlined,
   46: } from '@ant-design/icons';
   47: import type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';
   48: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
   49: 
   50: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
   51: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
   52: import DataTable from '@/components/common/DataTable';
   53: import StatusTag from '@/components/common/StatusTag';
   54: import DateDisplay from '@/components/common/DateDisplay';
   55: import UserAvatar from '@/components/common/UserAvatar';
   56: import StatsCard from '@/components/common/StatsCard';
   57: import { useTranslation } from '@/hooks/useTranslation';
   58: 
   59: import { leadApi } from '@/services/api/leads';
   60: import { studentApi } from '@/services/api/students';
   61: import type {
   62:   Lead,
   63:   LeadDetail,
   64:   LeadStatus,
   65:   LeadCreatePayload,
   66:   LeadInteractionCreatePayload,
   67:   LeadPlacementTestCreatePayload,
   68:   InteractionType,
   69: } from '@/services/types/lead';
   70: 
   71: const { Option } = Select;
   72: const { TextArea } = Input;
   73: const { Text, Title } = Typography;
   74: 
   75: interface LeadStats {
   76:   total: number;
   77:   newCount: number;
   78:   queueCount: number;
   79:   consultedCount: number;
   80: }
   81: 
   82: const LeadListPage = () => {
   83:   const { t } = useTranslation();
   84: 
   85:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   86:   // STATE
   87:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   88:   const [data, setData] = useState<Lead[]>([]);
   89:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   90:   const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
   91:   const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
   92:   const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(20);
   93:   const [keyword, setKeyword] = useState('');
   94:   const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<LeadStatus | undefined>();
   95:   const [inQueueFilter, setInQueueFilter] = useState<boolean | undefined>();
   96: 
   97:   // Metrics
   98:   const [stats, setStats] = useState<LeadStats>({
   99:     total: 0,
  100:     newCount: 0,
  101:     queueCount: 0,
  102:     consultedCount: 0,
  103:   });
  104: 
  105:   // Modals & Drawer
  106:   const [createModalVisible, setCreateModalVisible] = useState(false);
  107:   const [assignModalVisible, setAssignModalVisible] = useState(false);
  108:   const [interactionModalVisible, setInteractionModalVisible] = useState(false);
  109:   const [placementTestModalVisible, setPlacementTestModalVisible] = useState(false);
  110:   const [convertModalVisible, setConvertModalVisible] = useState(false);
  111:   const [detailDrawerVisible, setDetailDrawerVisible] = useState(false);
  112: 
  113:   const [selectedLead, setSelectedLead] = useState<Lead | null>(null);
  114:   const [leadDetail, setLeadDetail] = useState<LeadDetail | null>(null);
  115:   const [detailLoading, setDetailLoading] = useState(false);
  116: 
  117:   const [createForm] = Form.useForm();
  118:   const [assignForm] = Form.useForm();
  119:   const [interactionForm] = Form.useForm();
  120:   const [placementTestForm] = Form.useForm();
  121:   const [convertForm] = Form.useForm();
  122: 
  123:   // Watch result for Interaction Form (để bắt buộc lý do từ chối nếu result = REJECTED)
  124:   const interactionResult = Form.useWatch('result', interactionForm);
  125:   const testStatus = Form.useWatch('testStatus', placementTestForm);
  126: 
  127:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  128:   // FETCH STATS
  129:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  130:   const fetchStats = useCallback(async () => {
  131:     try {
  132:       const [allRes, newRes, queueRes, consultedRes] = await Promise.all([
  133:         leadApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1 }),
  134:         leadApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, status: 'NEW' }),
  135:         leadApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, inQueue: true }),
  136:         leadApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, status: 'CONSULTED' }),
  137:       ]);
  138:       setStats({
  139:         total: allRes.totalElements,
  140:         newCount: newRes.totalElements,
  141:         queueCount: queueRes.totalElements,
  142:         consultedCount: consultedRes.totalElements,
  143:       });
  144:     } catch {
  145:       // Fallback silently if stats endpoint or params fail
  146:     }
  147:   }, []);
  148: 
  149:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  150:   // FETCH DATA
  151:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  152:   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
  153:     setLoading(true);
  154:     try {
  155:       const res = await leadApi.search({
  156:         search: keyword.trim() || undefined,
  157:         status: statusFilter,
  158:         inQueue: inQueueFilter,
  159:         page,
  160:         size: pageSize,
  161:         sortBy: 'createdAt',
  162:         direction: 'desc',
  163:       });
  164:       setData(res.content);
  165:       setTotal(res.totalElements);
  166:     } catch (err) {
  167:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  168:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách Lead');
  169:       setData([]);
  170:       setTotal(0);
  171:     } finally {
  172:       setLoading(false);
  173:     }
  174:   }, [page, pageSize, keyword, statusFilter, inQueueFilter]);
  175: 
  176:   useEffect(() => {
  177:     fetchData();
  178:   }, [fetchData]);
  179: 
  180:   useEffect(() => {
  181:     fetchStats();
  182:   }, [fetchStats]);
  183: 
  184:   // Reset page khi filter thay đổi
  185:   useEffect(() => {
  186:     setPage(0);
  187:   }, [keyword, statusFilter, inQueueFilter]);
  188: 
  189:   const handleRefresh = () => {
  190:     fetchData();
  191:     fetchStats();
  192:     toast.success('Đã cập nhật dữ liệu mới nhất');
  193:   };
  194: 
  195:   const handleResetFilters = () => {
  196:     setKeyword('');
  197:     setStatusFilter(undefined);
  198:     setInQueueFilter(undefined);
  199:     setPage(0);
  200:   };
  201: 
  202:   const loadLeadDetail = async (leadId: number) => {
  203:     setDetailLoading(true);
  204:     try {
  205:       const detail = await leadApi.getDetailById(leadId);
  206:       setLeadDetail(detail);
  207:     } catch (err) {
  208:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  209:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải chi tiết Lead');
  210:     } finally {
  211:       setDetailLoading(false);
  212:     }
  213:   };
  214: 
  215:   const handleOpenDetail = (lead: Lead) => {
  216:     setSelectedLead(lead);
  217:     setDetailDrawerVisible(true);
  218:     loadLeadDetail(lead.id);
  219:   };
  220: 
  221:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  222:   // HANDLERS
  223:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  224:   const handleCreateLead = async (values: LeadCreatePayload) => {
  225:     try {
  226:       await leadApi.create(values);
  227:       toast.success('Tiếp nhận Lead mới thành công');
  228:       setCreateModalVisible(false);
  229:       createForm.resetFields();
  230:       fetchData();
  231:       fetchStats();
  232:     } catch (err) {
  233:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  234:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Tạo Lead thất bại');
  235:     }
  236:   };
  237: 
  238:   const handleAssignConsultant = async (values: { consultantId: number; reason?: string }) => {
  239:     if (!selectedLead) return;
  240:     try {
  241:       await leadApi.assignConsultant(selectedLead.id, values);
  242:       toast.success('Phân công tư vấn viên thành công');
  243:       setAssignModalVisible(false);
  244:       assignForm.resetFields();
  245:       fetchData();
  246:       fetchStats();
  247:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadLeadDetail(selectedLead.id);
  248:     } catch (err) {
  249:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  250:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Phân công thất bại');
  251:     }
  252:   };
  253: 
  254:   const handleLogInteraction = async (values: LeadInteractionCreatePayload) => {
  255:     if (!selectedLead) return;
  256:     try {
  257:       await leadApi.logInteraction(selectedLead.id, values);
  258:       toast.success('Ghi nhận tương tác thành công');
  259:       setInteractionModalVisible(false);
  260:       interactionForm.resetFields();
  261:       fetchData();
  262:       fetchStats();
  263:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadLeadDetail(selectedLead.id);
  264:     } catch (err) {
  265:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  266:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Ghi nhận thất bại');
  267:     }
  268:   };
  269: 
  270:   const handleRecordPlacementTest = async (values: LeadPlacementTestCreatePayload) => {
  271:     if (!selectedLead) return;
  272:     try {
  273:       await leadApi.recordPlacementTest(selectedLead.id, values);
  274:       toast.success('Ghi nhận đánh giá đầu vào thành công');
  275:       setPlacementTestModalVisible(false);
  276:       placementTestForm.resetFields();
  277:       fetchData();
  278:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadLeadDetail(selectedLead.id);
  279:     } catch (err) {
  280:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  281:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Ghi nhận test thất bại');
  282:     }
  283:   };
  284: 
  285:   const handleConvertToStudent = async (values: { courseId?: number; classId?: number; note?: string }) => {
  286:     if (!selectedLead) return;
  287:     try {
  288:       const res = await studentApi.convertFromLead({
  289:         leadId: selectedLead.id,
  290:         courseId: values.courseId || selectedLead.courseId,
  291:         classId: values.classId,
  292:         note: values.note,
  293:       });
  294:       toast.success(`Chuyển đổi thành công! Mã học viên: ${res.studentCode}`);
  295:       setConvertModalVisible(false);
  296:       convertForm.resetFields();
  297:       fetchData();
  298:       fetchStats();
  299:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadLeadDetail(selectedLead.id);
  300:     } catch (err) {
  301:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  302:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Chuyển đổi học viên thất bại');
  303:     }
  304:   };
  305: 
  306:   const handleCloseLead = async (id: number) => {
  307:     try {
  308:       await leadApi.delete(id);
  309:       toast.success('Đã cập nhật đóng tư vấn Lead');
  310:       fetchData();
  311:       fetchStats();
  312:     } catch (err) {
  313:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  314:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Thao tác thất bại');
  315:     }
  316:   };
  317: 
  318:   // Helper helper icons for interaction types
  319:   const renderInteractionIcon = (type: InteractionType) => {
  320:     switch (type) {
  321:       case 'CALL':
  322:         return <PhoneOutlined style={{ color: '#1677ff' }} />;
  323:       case 'MEETING':
  324:         return <TeamOutlined style={{ color: '#52c41a' }} />;
  325:       case 'CHAT':
  326:         return <MessageOutlined style={{ color: '#722ed1' }} />;
  327:       case 'EMAIL':
  328:         return <MailOutlined style={{ color: '#fa8c16' }} />;
  329:       default:
  330:         return <CommentOutlined style={{ color: '#8c8c8c' }} />;
  331:     }
  332:   };
  333: 
  334:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  335:   // COLUMNS
  336:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  337:   const columns: ColumnsType<Lead> = useMemo(
  338:     () => [
  339:       {
  340:         title: t('lead.code'),
  341:         dataIndex: 'id',
  342:         width: 90,
  343:         render: (id: number, record: Lead) => (
  344:           <Tag
  345:             color="default"
  346:             style={{
  347:               fontFamily: 'monospace',
  348:               fontWeight: 700,
  349:               fontSize: 12,
  350:               borderRadius: 6,
  351:               padding: '2px 8px',
  352:               cursor: 'pointer',
  353:               background: '#f8fafc',
  354:               borderColor: '#e2e8f0',
  355:               color: '#475569',
  356:             }}
  357:             onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  358:           >
  359:             #{String(id).padStart(4, '0')}
  360:           </Tag>
  361:         ),
  362:       },
  363:       {
  364:         title: t('lead.customer'),
  365:         key: 'customer',
  366:         width: 260,
  367:         render: (_, record: Lead) => (
  368:           <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
  369:             <UserAvatar name={record.fullName} size={36} />
  370:             <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
  371:               <div
  372:                 style={{
  373:                   fontWeight: 600,
  374:                   fontSize: 14,
  375:                   color: '#0f172a',
  376:                   cursor: 'pointer',
  377:                   lineHeight: 1.3,
  378:                   transition: 'color 0.15s ease',
  379:                 }}
  380:                 onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#1677ff')}
  381:                 onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.color = '#0f172a')}
  382:                 onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  383:               >
  384:                 {record.fullName}
  385:               </div>
  386:               <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginTop: 3 }}>
  387:                 {record.phone && (
  388:                   <a
  389:                     href={`tel:${record.phone}`}
  390:                     style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3 }}
  391:                   >
  392:                     <PhoneOutlined style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }} />
  393:                     {record.phone}
  394:                   </a>
  395:                 )}
  396:                 {record.email && (
  397:                   <a
  398:                     href={`mailto:${record.email}`}
  399:                     style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3 }}
  400:                   >
  401:                     <MailOutlined style={{ color: '#94a3b8' }} />
  402:                     {record.email}
  403:                   </a>
  404:                 )}
  405:               </div>
  406:             </div>
  407:           </div>
  408:         ),
  409:       },
  410:       {
  411:         title: t('lead.courseInterest'),
  412:         dataIndex: 'courseId',
  413:         width: 140,
  414:         render: (courseId?: number) =>
  415:           courseId ? (
  416:             <Tag
  417:               icon={<BookOutlined />}
  418:               color="processing"
  419:               style={{ borderRadius: 6, fontWeight: 500, padding: '2px 8px' }}
  420:             >
  421:               Course #{courseId}
  422:             </Tag>
  423:           ) : (
  424:             <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
  425:               —
  426:             </Text>
  427:           ),
  428:       },
  429:       {
  430:         title: t('lead.consultant'),
  431:         key: 'consultant',
  432:         width: 170,
  433:         render: (_, record: Lead) =>
  434:           record.assignedConsultantId ? (
  435:             <Tag
  436:               color="blue"
  437:               icon={<UserOutlined />}
  438:               style={{ borderRadius: 12, padding: '2px 10px', fontWeight: 500 }}
  439:             >
  440:               TVV #{record.assignedConsultantId}
  441:             </Tag>
  442:           ) : (
  443:             <Tag
  444:               color="warning"
  445:               style={{
  446:                 borderRadius: 12,
  447:                 padding: '2px 10px',
  448:                 fontWeight: 500,
  449:                 display: 'inline-flex',
  450:                 alignItems: 'center',
  451:               }}
  452:             >
  453:               <span className="pulse-dot-amber" />
  454:               {t('lead.unassigned')}
  455:             </Tag>
  456:           ),
  457:       },
  458:       {
  459:         title: t('common.status'),
  460:         dataIndex: 'status',
  461:         width: 130,
  462:         render: (status: LeadStatus) => <StatusTag status={status} />,
  463:       },
  464:       {
  465:         title: t('lead.converted'),
  466:         dataIndex: 'studentId',
  467:         width: 160,
  468:         render: (studentId?: number) =>
  469:           studentId ? (
  470:             <Tag
  471:               color="cyan"
  472:               icon={<CheckCircleOutlined />}
  473:               style={{ borderRadius: 12, fontWeight: 600, padding: '2px 10px' }}
  474:             >
  475:               HV #{studentId}
  476:             </Tag>
  477:           ) : (
  478:             <span
  479:               style={{
  480:                 fontSize: 12,
  481:                 color: '#94a3b8',
  482:                 padding: '2px 8px',
  483:                 background: '#f8fafc',
  484:                 borderRadius: 10,
  485:                 border: '1px dashed #cbd5e1',
  486:               }}
  487:             >
  488:               {t('lead.notConverted')}
  489:             </span>
  490:           ),
  491:       },
  492:       {
  493:         title: t('common.createdAt'),
  494:         dataIndex: 'createdAt',
  495:         width: 140,
  496:         render: (date: string) => <DateDisplay value={date} format="short" />,
  497:       },
  498:       {
  499:         title: t('common.actions'),
  500:         key: 'actions',
  501:         fixed: 'right',
  502:         width: 200,
  503:         render: (_, record: Lead) => (
  504:           <Space size={3}>
  505:             <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.view')}>
  506:               <Button
  507:                 type="text"
  508:                 shape="circle"
  509:                 className="action-btn-circle view"
  510:                 icon={<EyeOutlined style={{ color: '#1677ff', fontSize: 15 }} />}
  511:                 onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  512:               />
  513:             </Tooltip>
  514: 
  515:             <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.assign')}>
  516:               <Button
  517:                 type="text"
  518:                 shape="circle"
  519:                 className="action-btn-circle assign"
  520:                 icon={<UserSwitchOutlined style={{ color: '#722ed1', fontSize: 15 }} />}
  521:                 onClick={() => {
  522:                   setSelectedLead(record);
  523:                   assignForm.setFieldsValue({ consultantId: record.assignedConsultantId });
  524:                   setAssignModalVisible(true);
  525:                 }}
  526:               />
  527:             </Tooltip>
  528: 
  529:             <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.interact')}>
  530:               <Button
  531:                 type="text"
  532:                 shape="circle"
  533:                 className="action-btn-circle interact"
  534:                 icon={<CommentOutlined style={{ color: '#fa8c16', fontSize: 15 }} />}
  535:                 onClick={() => {
  536:                   setSelectedLead(record);
  537:                   interactionForm.resetFields();
  538:                   setInteractionModalVisible(true);
  539:                 }}
  540:               />
  541:             </Tooltip>
  542: 
  543:             <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.test')}>
  544:               <Button
  545:                 type="text"
  546:                 shape="circle"
  547:                 className="action-btn-circle test"
  548:                 icon={<FormOutlined style={{ color: '#08979c', fontSize: 15 }} />}
  549:                 onClick={() => {
  550:                   setSelectedLead(record);
  551:                   placementTestForm.resetFields();
  552:                   setPlacementTestModalVisible(true);
  553:                 }}
  554:               />
  555:             </Tooltip>
  556: 
  557:             {!record.studentId && (
  558:               <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.convert')}>
  559:                 <Button
  560:                   type="primary"
  561:                   size="small"
  562:                   shape="round"
  563:                   className="btn-success-gradient"
  564:                   icon={<CheckCircleOutlined />}
  565:                   style={{
  566:                     fontSize: 12,
  567:                     fontWeight: 600,
  568:                     padding: '0 10px',
  569:                     height: 26,
  570:                   }}
  571:                   onClick={() => {
  572:                     setSelectedLead(record);
  573:                     convertForm.setFieldsValue({ courseId: record.courseId });
  574:                     setConvertModalVisible(true);
  575:                   }}
  576:                 >
  577:                   {t('lead.convertBtn')}
  578:                 </Button>
  579:               </Tooltip>
  580:             )}
  581: 
  582:             <Popconfirm
  583:               title={t('lead.popconfirm.closeTitle')}
  584:               description={t('lead.popconfirm.closeDesc')}
  585:               onConfirm={() => handleCloseLead(record.id)}
  586:               okText={t('common.confirm')}
  587:               cancelText={t('common.cancel')}
  588:               okButtonProps={{ danger: true }}
  589:             >
  590:               <Tooltip title={t('lead.tooltip.close')}>
  591:                 <Button
  592:                   type="text"
  593:                   danger
  594:                   shape="circle"
  595:                   className="action-btn-circle delete"
  596:                   icon={<CloseCircleOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  597:                 />
  598:               </Tooltip>
  599:             </Popconfirm>
  600:           </Space>
  601:         ),
  602:       },
  603:     ],
  604:     [t, handleOpenDetail]
  605:   );
  606: 
  607:   const hasActiveFilters = Boolean(keyword || statusFilter || inQueueFilter !== undefined);
  608: 
  609:   return (
  610:     <PageContainer>
  611:       <PageHeader
  612:         title={t('lead.title')}
  613:         subtitle={t('lead.subtitle')}
  614:         badge={
  615:           <Tag
  616:             color="blue"
  617:             style={{
  618:               borderRadius: 12,
  619:               fontWeight: 600,
  620:               fontSize: 12,
  621:               padding: '2px 10px',
  622:               border: 'none',
  623:               background: '#eff6ff',
  624:               color: '#2563eb',
  625:             }}
  626:           >
  627:             CRM & Admissions
  628:           </Tag>
  629:         }
  630:         extra={
  631:           <Space>
  632:             <Button
  633:               icon={<ReloadOutlined spin={loading} />}
  634:               onClick={handleRefresh}
  635:               style={{ borderRadius: 8, height: 38, fontWeight: 500 }}
  636:             >
  637:               {t('common.refresh')}
  638:             </Button>
  639:             <Button
  640:               type="primary"
  641:               icon={<PlusOutlined />}
  642:               onClick={() => setCreateModalVisible(true)}
  643:               className="btn-primary-gradient"
  644:               style={{

  122:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  123:   const fetchStats = useCallback(async () => {
  124:     try {
  125:       const [allRes, activeRes, inactiveRes] = await Promise.all([
  126:         studentApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1 }),
  127:         studentApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, status: 'ACTIVE' }),
  128:         studentApi.search({ page: 0, size: 1, status: 'INACTIVE' }),
  129:       ]);
  130:       setStats({
  131:         total: allRes.totalElements,
  132:         activeCount: activeRes.totalElements,
  133:         inactiveCount: inactiveRes.totalElements,
  134:       });
  135:     } catch {
  136:       // Fallback silently
  137:     }
  138:   }, []);
  139: 
  140:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  141:   // FETCH DATA
  142:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  143:   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
  144:     setLoading(true);
  145:     try {
  146:       const res = await studentApi.search({
  147:         search: keyword.trim() || undefined,
  148:         status: statusFilter,
  149:         page,
  150:         size: pageSize,
  151:         sortBy: 'createdAt',
  152:         direction: 'desc',
  153:       });
  154:       setData(res.content);
  155:       setTotal(res.totalElements);
  156:     } catch (err) {
  157:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  158:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách học viên');
  159:       setData([]);
  160:       setTotal(0);
  161:     } finally {
  162:       setLoading(false);
  163:     }
  164:   }, [page, pageSize, keyword, statusFilter]);
  165: 
  166:   useEffect(() => {
  167:     fetchData();
  168:   }, [fetchData]);
  169: 
  170:   useEffect(() => {
  171:     fetchStats();
  172:   }, [fetchStats]);
  173: 
  174:   useEffect(() => {
  175:     setPage(0);
  176:   }, [keyword, statusFilter]);
  177: 
  178:   const handleRefresh = () => {
  179:     fetchData();
  180:     fetchStats();
  181:     toast.success('Đã làm mới dữ liệu học viên');
  182:   };
  183: 
  184:   const handleResetFilters = () => {
  185:     setKeyword('');
  186:     setStatusFilter(undefined);
  187:     setPage(0);
  188:   };
  189: 
  190:   const loadStudentDetail = async (id: number) => {
  191:     setDetailLoading(true);
  192:     try {
  193:       const detail = await studentApi.getDetailById(id);
  194:       setStudentDetail(detail);
  195:     } catch (err) {
  196:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  197:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải chi tiết học viên');
  198:     } finally {
  199:       setDetailLoading(false);
  200:     }
  201:   };
  202: 
  203:   const handleOpenDetail = (student: Student) => {
  204:     setSelectedStudent(student);
  205:     setDetailDrawerVisible(true);
  206:     loadStudentDetail(student.id);
  207:   };
  208: 
  209:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  210:   // HANDLERS
  211:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  212:   const handleCreateStudent = async (values: StudentCreatePayload) => {
  213:     try {
  214:       const res = await studentApi.create(values);
  215:       toast.success(`Tạo học viên thành công! Mã: ${res.studentCode}`);
  216:       setCreateModalVisible(false);
  217:       createForm.resetFields();
  218:       fetchData();
  219:       fetchStats();
  220:     } catch (err) {
  221:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  222:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Tạo học viên thất bại');
  223:     }
  224:   };
  225: 
  226:   const handleUpdateStudent = async (values: StudentUpdatePayload) => {
  227:     if (!selectedStudent) return;
  228:     try {
  229:       await studentApi.update(selectedStudent.id, values);
  230:       toast.success('Cập nhật thông tin học viên thành công');
  231:       setUpdateModalVisible(false);
  232:       updateForm.resetFields();
  233:       fetchData();
  234:       fetchStats();
  235:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadStudentDetail(selectedStudent.id);
  236:     } catch (err) {
  237:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  238:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Cập nhật thất bại');
  239:     }
  240:   };
  241: 
  242:   const handleUpdateStatus = async (values: { status: StudentStatus; reason: string }) => {
  243:     if (!selectedStudent) return;
  244:     try {
  245:       await studentApi.updateStatus(selectedStudent.id, values);
  246:       toast.success('Cập nhật trạng thái thành công');
  247:       setStatusModalVisible(false);
  248:       statusForm.resetFields();
  249:       fetchData();
  250:       fetchStats();
  251:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadStudentDetail(selectedStudent.id);
  252:     } catch (err) {
  253:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  254:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Đổi trạng thái thất bại');
  255:     }
  256:   };
  257: 
  258:   const handleAddGuardian = async (values: GuardianPayload) => {
  259:     if (!selectedStudent) return;
  260:     try {
  261:       await studentApi.addGuardian(selectedStudent.id, values);
  262:       toast.success('Thêm người giám hộ thành công');
  263:       setGuardianModalVisible(false);
  264:       guardianForm.resetFields();
  265:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadStudentDetail(selectedStudent.id);
  266:     } catch (err) {
  267:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  268:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Thêm người giám hộ thất bại');
  269:     }
  270:   };
  271: 
  272:   const handleDeleteGuardian = async (guardianId: number) => {
  273:     if (!selectedStudent) return;
  274:     try {
  275:       await studentApi.deleteGuardian(selectedStudent.id, guardianId);
  276:       toast.success('Đã xóa người giám hộ');
  277:       if (detailDrawerVisible) loadStudentDetail(selectedStudent.id);
  278:     } catch (err) {
  279:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  280:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Xóa người giám hộ thất bại');
  281:     }
  282:   };
  283: 
  284:   const handleDeleteStudent = async (id: number) => {
  285:     try {
  286:       await studentApi.delete(id);
  287:       toast.success('Đã chuyển học viên sang trạng thái INACTIVE');
  288:       fetchData();
  289:       fetchStats();
  290:     } catch (err) {
  291:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  292:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Thao tác thất bại');
  293:     }
  294:   };
  295: 
  296:   const handleLockAccount = async (values: { reason?: string }) => {
  297:     if (!selectedStudent) return;
  298:     setLockLoading(true);
  299:     try {
  300:       await studentApi.lockAccount(selectedStudent.id, {
  301:         isLocked: isLockingAction,
  302:         reason: values.reason?.trim() || undefined,
  303:       });
  304:       toast.success(
  305:         isLockingAction
  306:           ? 'Đã khóa tài khoản học viên và vô hiệu hóa các phiên đăng nhập'
  307:           : 'Đã mở khóa tài khoản học viên thành công'
  308:       );
  309:       setAccountLockModalVisible(false);
  310:       accountLockForm.resetFields();
  311:       fetchData();
  312:       fetchStats();
  313:       if (detailDrawerVisible) {
  314:         loadStudentDetail(selectedStudent.id);
  315:       }
  316:     } catch (err) {
  317:       const e = err as { message?: string };
  318:       toast.error(
  319:         e?.message ||
  320:           (isLockingAction ? 'Khóa tài khoản thất bại' : 'Mở khóa thất bại')
  321:       );
  322:     } finally {
  323:       setLockLoading(false);
  324:     }
  325:   };
  326: 
  327:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  328:   // COLUMNS
  329:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  330:   const columns: ColumnsType<Student> = useMemo(
  331:     () => [
  332:       {
  333:         title: t('student.code'),
  334:         dataIndex: 'studentCode',
  335:         width: 150,
  336:         render: (code: string, record: Student) => (
  337:           <Tag
  338:             color="blue"
  339:             style={{
  340:               fontFamily: 'monospace',
  341:               fontWeight: 700,
  342:               fontSize: 13,
  343:               borderRadius: 6,
  344:               padding: '3px 8px',
  345:               cursor: 'pointer',
  346:               border: '1px solid #bfdbfe',
  347:               background: '#eff6ff',
  348:               color: '#1d4ed8',
  349:               letterSpacing: '0.02em',
  350:               transition: 'all 0.2s',
  351:             }}
  352:             onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  353:           >
  354:             {code}
  355:           </Tag>
  356:         ),
  357:       },
  358:       {
  359:         title: t('student.fullName'),
  360:         key: 'student',
  361:         width: 250,
  362:         render: (_, record: Student) => (
  363:           <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
  364:             <UserAvatar name={record.fullName} size={38} />
  365:             <div style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
  366:               <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
  367:                 <span
  368:                   style={{
  369:                     fontWeight: 600,
  370:                     fontSize: 14,
  371:                     color: '#0f172a',
  372:                     cursor: 'pointer',
  373:                     lineHeight: 1.3,
  374:                     transition: 'color 0.2s',
  375:                   }}
  376:                   onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  377:                 >
  378:                   {record.fullName}
  379:                 </span>
  380:                 {record.isAccountLocked && (
  381:                   <Tooltip title={t('student.accountLocked')}>
  382:                     <Tag
  383:                       color="error"
  384:                       style={{
  385:                         borderRadius: 10,
  386:                         padding: '0 6px',
  387:                         fontSize: 11,
  388:                         margin: 0,
  389:                         lineHeight: '18px',
  390:                         display: 'inline-flex',
  391:                         alignItems: 'center',
  392:                         gap: 2,
  393:                       }}
  394:                     >
  395:                       <LockOutlined style={{ fontSize: 10 }} />
  396:                       <span>{t('user.locked')}</span>
  397:                     </Tag>
  398:                   </Tooltip>
  399:                 )}
  400:               </div>
  401:               <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10, marginTop: 3 }}>
  402:                 {record.phone && (
  403:                   <a
  404:                     href={`tel:${record.phone}`}
  405:                     style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3 }}
  406:                     onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
  407:                   >
  408:                     <PhoneOutlined style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8' }} />
  409:                     <span>{record.phone}</span>
  410:                   </a>
  411:                 )}
  412:                 {record.email && (
  413:                   <a
  414:                     href={`mailto:${record.email}`}
  415:                     style={{ fontSize: 12, color: '#64748b', display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 3 }}
  416:                     onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
  417:                   >
  418:                     <MailOutlined style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#94a3b8' }} />
  419:                     <span>{record.email}</span>
  420:                   </a>
  421:                 )}
  422:               </div>
  423:             </div>
  424:           </div>
  425:         ),
  426:       },
  427:       {
  428:         title: t('student.dob'),
  429:         dataIndex: 'dateOfBirth',
  430:         width: 130,
  431:         render: (dob?: string) =>
  432:           dob ? (
  433:             <Space size={4} style={{ color: '#475569', fontSize: 13 }}>
  434:               <CalendarOutlined style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 12 }} />
  435:               <span>{dob}</span>
  436:             </Space>
  437:           ) : (
  438:             <Text type="secondary">—</Text>
  439:           ),
  440:       },
  441:       {
  442:         title: t('common.status'),
  443:         dataIndex: 'status',
  444:         width: 140,
  445:         render: (status: StudentStatus) => <StatusTag status={status} />,
  446:       },
  447:       {
  448:         title: t('student.origin'),
  449:         dataIndex: 'leadId',
  450:         width: 150,
  451:         render: (leadId?: number) =>
  452:           leadId ? (
  453:             <Tag
  454:               color="cyan"
  455:               icon={<LinkOutlined />}
  456:               style={{
  457:                 borderRadius: 12,
  458:                 padding: '2px 9px',
  459:                 fontWeight: 500,
  460:                 border: 'none',
  461:                 background: '#ecfeff',
  462:                 color: '#0e7490',
  463:               }}
  464:             >
  465:               {t('student.fromLead')} #{leadId}
  466:             </Tag>
  467:           ) : (
  468:             <Tag
  469:               color="default"
  470:               style={{
  471:                 borderRadius: 12,
  472:                 padding: '2px 9px',
  473:                 fontWeight: 500,
  474:                 border: 'none',
  475:                 background: '#f1f5f9',
  476:                 color: '#475569',
  477:               }}
  478:             >
  479:               {t('student.direct')}
  480:             </Tag>
  481:           ),
  482:       },
  483:       {
  484:         title: t('student.enrollDate'),
  485:         dataIndex: 'createdAt',
  486:         width: 140,
  487:         render: (date: string) => <DateDisplay value={date} format="short" />,
  488:       },
  489:       {
  490:         title: t('common.actions'),
  491:         key: 'actions',
  492:         fixed: 'right',
  493:         width: 230,
  494:         render: (_, record: Student) => (
  495:           <Space size={4}>
  496:             <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.view')}>
  497:               <Button
  498:                 type="text"
  499:                 shape="circle"
  500:                 className="action-btn-circle view"
  501:                 icon={<EyeOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  502:                 onClick={() => handleOpenDetail(record)}
  503:               />
  504:             </Tooltip>
  505: 
  506:             <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.edit')}>
  507:               <Button
  508:                 type="text"
  509:                 shape="circle"
  510:                 className="action-btn-circle edit"
  511:                 icon={<EditOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  512:                 onClick={() => {
  513:                   setSelectedStudent(record);
  514:                   updateForm.setFieldsValue({
  515:                     fullName: record.fullName,
  516:                     phone: record.phone,
  517:                     email: record.email,
  518:                     dateOfBirth: record.dateOfBirth,
  519:                     address: record.address,
  520:                   });
  521:                   setUpdateModalVisible(true);
  522:                 }}
  523:               />
  524:             </Tooltip>
  525: 
  526:             {/* Khóa / Mở khóa tài khoản học viên */}
  527:             {record.isAccountLocked ? (
  528:               <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.unlockAccount')}>
  529:                 <Button
  530:                   type="text"
  531:                   shape="circle"
  532:                   className="action-btn-circle"
  533:                   style={{
  534:                     color: '#16a34a',
  535:                     background: '#dcfce7',
  536:                     border: '1px solid #bbf7d0',
  537:                   }}
  538:                   icon={<UnlockOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  539:                   onClick={() => {
  540:                     setSelectedStudent(record);
  541:                     setIsLockingAction(false);
  542:                     accountLockForm.resetFields();
  543:                     setAccountLockModalVisible(true);
  544:                   }}
  545:                 />
  546:               </Tooltip>
  547:             ) : (
  548:               <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.lockAccount')}>
  549:                 <Button
  550:                   type="text"
  551:                   shape="circle"
  552:                   className="action-btn-circle"
  553:                   style={{
  554:                     color: '#d97706',
  555:                     background: '#fef3c7',
  556:                     border: '1px solid #fde68a',
  557:                   }}
  558:                   icon={<LockOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  559:                   onClick={() => {
  560:                     setSelectedStudent(record);
  561:                     setIsLockingAction(true);
  562:                     accountLockForm.resetFields();
  563:                     setAccountLockModalVisible(true);
  564:                   }}
  565:                 />
  566:               </Tooltip>
  567:             )}
  568: 
  569:             <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.changeStatus')}>
  570:               <Button
  571:                 type="text"
  572:                 shape="circle"
  573:                 className="action-btn-circle assign"
  574:                 icon={<SyncOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  575:                 onClick={() => {
  576:                   setSelectedStudent(record);
  577:                   statusForm.setFieldsValue({
  578:                     status: record.status,
  579:                   });
  580:                   setStatusModalVisible(true);
  581:                 }}
  582:               />
  583:             </Tooltip>
  584: 
  585:             <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.addGuardian')}>
  586:               <Button
  587:                 type="text"
  588:                 shape="circle"
  589:                 className="action-btn-circle interact"
  590:                 icon={<UsergroupAddOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  591:                 onClick={() => {
  592:                   setSelectedStudent(record);
  593:                   guardianForm.resetFields();
  594:                   setGuardianModalVisible(true);
  595:                 }}
  596:               />
  597:             </Tooltip>
  598: 
  599:             <Popconfirm
  600:               title={t('student.popconfirm.inactivateTitle')}
  601:               description={t('student.popconfirm.inactivateDesc')}
  602:               onConfirm={() => handleDeleteStudent(record.id)}
  603:               okText={t('common.confirm')}
  604:               cancelText={t('common.cancel')}
  605:               okButtonProps={{ danger: true }}
  606:             >
  607:               <Tooltip title={t('student.tooltip.inactivate')}>
  608:                 <Button
  609:                   type="text"
  610:                   danger
  611:                   shape="circle"
  612:                   className="action-btn-circle delete"
  613:                   icon={<DeleteOutlined style={{ fontSize: 15 }} />}
  614:                 />
  615:               </Tooltip>
  616:             </Popconfirm>
  617:           </Space>
  618:         ),
  619:       },
  620:     ],
  621:     [t, updateForm, statusForm, guardianForm, accountLockForm]
  622:   );
  623: 
  624:   const hasActiveFilters = Boolean(keyword || statusFilter);
  625: 
  626:   return (
  627:     <PageContainer>
  628:       <PageHeader
  629:         title={t('student.title')}
  630:         subtitle={t('student.subtitle')}
  631:         badge={
  632:           <Tag
  633:             color="purple"
  634:             style={{
  635:               borderRadius: 12,
  636:               fontWeight: 600,
  637:               fontSize: 12,
  638:               padding: '2px 10px',
  639:               border: 'none',
  640:               background: '#f5f3ff',
  641:               color: '#7c3aed',
  642:             }}
  643:           >
  644:             Academics & Students
  645:           </Tag>
  646:         }
  647:         extra={
  648:           <Space>
  649:             <Button
  650:               icon={<ReloadOutlined spin={loading} />}
  651:               onClick={handleRefresh}
  652:               style={{ borderRadius: 8, height: 38, fontWeight: 500 }}
  653:             >
  654:               {t('common.refresh')}
  655:             </Button>
  656:             <Button
  657:               type="primary"
  658:               icon={<PlusOutlined />}
  659:               onClick={() => setCreateModalVisible(true)}
  660:               className="btn-primary-gradient"
  661:               style={{
  662:                 borderRadius: 8,
  663:                 height: 38,
  664:                 fontWeight: 500,
  665:                 border: 'none',
  666:               }}
  667:             >
  668:               {t('student.create')}
  669:             </Button>
  670:           </Space>
  671:         }
  672:       />
  673: 
  674:       {/* ── KPI Metric Cards ── */}
  675:       <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
  676:         <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>
  677:           <StatsCard
  678:             title={t('student.total')}
  679:             value={stats.total}
  680:             icon={<UserOutlined />}
  681:             color="#2563eb"
  682:             bgColor="#eff6ff"
  683:             active={!statusFilter}
  684:             onClick={() => setStatusFilter(undefined)}
  685:             tooltip={t('student.stats.allTooltip')}
  686:           />
  687:         </Col>
  688:         <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>
  689:           <StatsCard
  690:             title={t('student.active')}
  691:             value={stats.activeCount}
  692:             icon={<CheckCircleOutlined />}
  693:             color="#10b981"
  694:             bgColor="#ecfdf5"
  695:             active={statusFilter === 'ACTIVE'}
  696:             onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'ACTIVE' ? undefined : 'ACTIVE')}
  697:             tagText={t('status.ACTIVE')}
  698:             tagColor="success"
  699:             tooltip={t('student.stats.activeTooltip')}
  700:           />
  701:         </Col>
  702:         <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={8}>
  703:           <StatsCard
  704:             title={t('student.inactive')}
  705:             value={stats.inactiveCount}
  706:             icon={<StopOutlined />}
  707:             color="#ef4444"
  708:             bgColor="#fef2f2"
  709:             active={statusFilter === 'INACTIVE'}
  710:             onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'INACTIVE' ? undefined : 'INACTIVE')}
  711:             tagText={t('status.INACTIVE')}
  712:             tagColor="error"
  713:             tooltip={t('student.stats.inactiveTooltip')}
  714:           />
  715:         </Col>
  716:       </Row>
  717: 
  718:       {/* ── Table & Filters ── */}
  719:       <Card
  720:         bordered={false}
  721:         style={{
  722:           borderRadius: 14,
  723:           border: '1px solid #e2e8f0',
  724:           boxShadow: '0 4px 16px -2px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04)',
  725:           background: '#ffffff',
  726:           overflow: 'hidden',
  727:         }}
  728:         bodyStyle={{ padding: 18 }}
  729:       >
  730:         <DataTable
  731:           columns={columns}
  732:           dataSource={data}
  733:           loading={loading}
  734:           total={total}
  735:           page={page}
  736:           pageSize={pageSize}
  737:           onPageChange={(p, ps) => {
  738:             setPage(p);
  739:             setPageSize(ps);
  740:           }}
  741:           onSearch={setKeyword}
  742:           searchPlaceholder={t('student.filter.searchPlaceholder')}
  743:           extra={
  744:             <Space wrap size={10}>
  745:               <Select
  746:                 placeholder={t('student.filter.status')}
  747:                 allowClear

    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import {
    3:   Card, Table, Button, Space, Modal, Form, Input, Popconfirm, Tooltip, Tag,
    4: } from 'antd';
    5: import {
    6:   PlusOutlined, EditOutlined, DeleteOutlined, ReloadOutlined,
    7: } from '@ant-design/icons';
    8: import type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';
    9: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
   10: 
   11: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
   12: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
   13: import StatusTag from '@/components/common/StatusTag';
   14: import DateDisplay from '@/components/common/DateDisplay';
   15: 
   16: import { adminRoleApi } from '@/services/api/admin';
   17: import type { Role } from '@/services/types/user';
   18: 
   19: interface RoleFormValues {
   20:   code: string;
   21:   description?: string;
   22: }
   23: 
   24: const RoleManagePage = () => {
   25:   const [roles, setRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);
   26:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   27:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   28:   const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
   29:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Role | null>(null);
   30:   const [form] = Form.useForm<RoleFormValues>();
   31: 
   32:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   33:   // FETCH
   34:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   35:   const fetchData = async () => {
   36:     setLoading(true);
   37:     try {
   38:       const list = await adminRoleApi.list();
   39:       setRoles(list);
   40:     } catch (err) {
   41:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   42:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải danh sách vai trò');
   43:     } finally {
   44:       setLoading(false);
   45:     }
   46:   };
   47: 
   48:   useEffect(() => {
   49:     fetchData();
   50:   }, []);
   51: 
   52:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   53:   // HANDLERS
   54:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   55:   const openCreate = () => {
   56:     setEditing(null);
   57:     form.resetFields();
   58:     setModalOpen(true);
   59:   };
   60: 
   61:   const openEdit = (role: Role) => {
   62:     setEditing(role);
   63:     form.setFieldsValue({
   64:       code: role.code,
   65:       description: role.description,
   66:     });
   67:     setModalOpen(true);
   68:   };
   69: 
   70:   const handleSubmit = async () => {
   71:     try {
   72:       const values = await form.validateFields();
   73:       setSaving(true);
   74:       if (editing) {
   75:         await adminRoleApi.update(editing.id, values);
   76:         toast.success('Cập nhật vai trò thành công');
   77:       } else {
   78:         await adminRoleApi.create(values);
   79:         toast.success('Tạo vai trò thành công');
   80:       }
   81:       setModalOpen(false);
   82:       fetchData();
   83:     } catch (err) {
   84:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   85:       if (e?.message) toast.error(e.message);
   86:     } finally {
   87:       setSaving(false);
   88:     }
   89:   };
   90: 
   91:   const handleDelete = async (id: number) => {
   92:     try {
   93:       await adminRoleApi.delete(id);
   94:       toast.success('Xóa vai trò thành công');
   95:       fetchData();
   96:     } catch (err) {
   97:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   98:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Xóa thất bại');
   99:     }
  100:   };
  101: 
  102:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  103:   // COLUMNS
  104:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  105:   const columns: ColumnsType<Role> = [
  106:     {
  107:       title: 'ID',
  108:       dataIndex: 'id',
  109:       key: 'id',
  110:       width: 80,
  111:       align: 'center',
  112:     },
  113:     {
  114:       title: 'Mã vai trò',
  115:       dataIndex: 'code',
  116:       key: 'code',
  117:       width: 180,
  118:       render: (code: string) => <StatusTag status={code} />,
  119:     },
  120:     {
  121:       title: 'Mô tả',
  122:       dataIndex: 'description',
  123:       key: 'description',
  124:       ellipsis: true,
  125:       render: (v?: string) => v || <Tag>—</Tag>,
  126:     },
  127:     {
  128:       title: 'Ngày tạo',
  129:       dataIndex: 'createdAt',
  130:       key: 'createdAt',
  131:       width: 160,
  132:       render: (v?: string) => <DateDisplay value={v} format="long" />,
  133:     },
  134:     {
  135:       title: 'Thao tác',
  136:       key: 'action',
  137:       width: 130,
  138:       align: 'center',
  139:       fixed: 'right',
  140:       render: (_, record) => (
  141:         <Space size={4}>
  142:           <Tooltip title="Sửa">
  143:             <Button
  144:               type="text"
  145:               icon={<EditOutlined />}
  146:               onClick={() => openEdit(record)}
  147:             />
  148:           </Tooltip>
  149:           <Popconfirm
  150:             title="Xóa vai trò"
  151:             description={`Bạn có chắc muốn xóa vai trò "${record.code}"?`}
  152:             okText="Xóa"
  153:             cancelText="Hủy"
  154:             okButtonProps={{ danger: true }}
  155:             onConfirm={() => handleDelete(record.id)}
  156:           >
  157:             <Tooltip title="Xóa">
  158:               <Button type="text" danger icon={<DeleteOutlined />} />
  159:             </Tooltip>
  160:           </Popconfirm>
  161:         </Space>
  162:       ),
  163:     },
  164:   ];
  165: 
  166:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  167:   // RENDER
  168:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  169:   return (
  170:     <PageContainer>
  171:       <PageHeader
  172:         title="Quản lý vai trò"
  173:         subtitle={`Tổng ${roles.length} vai trò trong hệ thống`}
  174:         extra={
  175:           <>
  176:             <Button
  177:               icon={<ReloadOutlined />}
  178:               onClick={() => {
  179:                 fetchData();
  180:                 toast.success('Đã làm mới');
  181:               }}
  182:             >
  183:               Làm mới
  184:             </Button>
  185:             <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={openCreate}>
  186:               Tạo vai trò
  187:             </Button>
  188:           </>
  189:         }
  190:       />
  191: 
  192:       <Card>
  193:         <Table<Role>
  194:           columns={columns}
  195:           dataSource={roles}
  196:           loading={loading}
  197:           rowKey="id"
  198:           pagination={false}
  199:           scroll={{ x: 'max-content' }}
  200:         />
  201:       </Card>
  202: 
  203:       {/* Modal create/edit */}
  204:       <Modal
  205:         title={editing ? `Sửa vai trò #${editing.id}` : 'Tạo vai trò mới'}
  206:         open={modalOpen}
  207:         onCancel={() => setModalOpen(false)}
  208:         onOk={handleSubmit}
  209:         confirmLoading={saving}
  210:         okText={editing ? 'Cập nhật' : 'Tạo mới'}
  211:         cancelText="Hủy"
  212:         destroyOnClose
  213:       >
  214:         <Form form={form} layout="vertical" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
  215:           <Form.Item
  216:             name="code"
  217:             label="Mã vai trò"
  218:             rules={[
  219:               { required: true, message: 'Vui lòng nhập mã vai trò' },
  220:               { max: 50, message: 'Tối đa 50 ký tự' },
  221:               { pattern: /^[A-Z_]+$/, message: 'Chỉ chữ in hoa và dấu _' },
  222:             ]}
  223:           >
  224:             <Input placeholder="VD: ADMIN, INSTRUCTOR, STUDENT" />
  225:           </Form.Item>
  226: 
  227:           <Form.Item
  228:             name="description"
  229:             label="Mô tả"
  230:             rules={[{ max: 255, message: 'Tối đa 255 ký tự' }]}
  231:           >
  232:             <Input.TextArea rows={3} placeholder="Mô tả vai trò này..." />
  233:           </Form.Item>
  234:         </Form>
  235:       </Modal>
  236:     </PageContainer>
  237:   );
  238: };
  239: 
  240: export default RoleManagePage;

// FILE: ./src/pages/admin/system/SystemLogPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
    2: import { Space, Select, Tag, Typography, Tooltip, Modal, Button } from 'antd';
    3: import { EyeOutlined, ReloadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
    4: import type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';
    5: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
    6: 
    7: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
    8: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
    9: import DataTable from '@/components/common/DataTable';
   10: import DateDisplay from '@/components/common/DateDisplay';
   11: 
   12: import { auditLogApi } from '@/services/api/admin';
   13: import type { AuditLogResponse } from '@/services/types/admin';
   14: 
   15: const { Text } = Typography;
   16: 
   17: const ENTITY_TYPES = [
   18:   'COURSE', 'RESOURCE', 'SESSION', 'QUESTION', 'ROOM', 'CATEGORY',
   19:   'ACCOUNT', 'ROLE', 'PERMISSION',
   20: ];
   21: 
   22: const ACTIONS = [
   23:   'CREATE', 'UPDATE', 'DELETE', 'SOFT_DELETE', 'HARD_DELETE',
   24:   'APPROVE', 'REJECT', 'CANCEL', 'ARCHIVE', 'SUBMIT_REVIEW', 'COPY',
   25: ];
   26: 
   27: const SystemLogPage = () => {
   28:   const [data, setData] = useState<AuditLogResponse[]>([]);
   29:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
   30:   const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
   31:   const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
   32:   const [pageSize, setPageSize] = useState(20);
   33:   const [entityTypeFilter, setEntityTypeFilter] = useState<string | undefined>();
   34:   const [actionFilter, setActionFilter] = useState<string | undefined>();
   35:   const [detailLog, setDetailLog] = useState<AuditLogResponse | null>(null);
   36: 
   37:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   38:   // FETCH
   39:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   40:   const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
   41:     setLoading(true);
   42:     try {
   43:       const params: Record<string, unknown> = { page, size: pageSize };
   44:       if (entityTypeFilter) params.entityType = entityTypeFilter;
   45:       if (actionFilter) params.action = actionFilter;
   46: 
   47:       const res = await auditLogApi.search(params);
   48:       setData(res.content);
   49:       setTotal(res.totalElements);
   50:     } catch (err) {
   51:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   52:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Không thể tải nhật ký');
   53:       setData([]);
   54:       setTotal(0);
   55:     } finally {
   56:       setLoading(false);
   57:     }
   58:   }, [page, pageSize, entityTypeFilter, actionFilter]);
   59: 
   60:   useEffect(() => {
   61:     fetchData();
   62:   }, [fetchData]);
   63: 
   64:   useEffect(() => {
   65:     setPage(0);
   66:   }, [entityTypeFilter, actionFilter]);
   67: 
   68:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   69:   // ACTION COLOR
   70:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   71:   const actionColor = (action: string): string => {
   72:     if (action.includes('DELETE') || action.includes('CANCEL') || action.includes('REJECT'))
   73:       return 'red';
   74:     if (action.includes('CREATE') || action.includes('APPROVE')) return 'green';
   75:     if (action.includes('UPDATE') || action.includes('SUBMIT')) return 'blue';
   76:     return 'default';
   77:   };
   78: 
   79:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   80:   // COLUMNS
   81:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   82:   const columns: ColumnsType<AuditLogResponse> = [
   83:     {
   84:       title: 'ID',
   85:       dataIndex: 'id',
   86:       key: 'id',
   87:       width: 80,
   88:       align: 'center',
   89:     },
   90:     {
   91:       title: 'Hành động',
   92:       dataIndex: 'action',
   93:       key: 'action',
   94:       width: 150,
   95:       render: (action: string) => (
   96:         <Tag color={actionColor(action)}>{action}</Tag>
   97:       ),
   98:     },
   99:     {
  100:       title: 'Đối tượng',
  101:       key: 'entity',
  102:       width: 180,
  103:       render: (_, record) => (
  104:         <Space size={4}>
  105:           <Tag color="blue">{record.entityType}</Tag>
  106:           {record.entityId && <Text code>#{record.entityId}</Text>}
  107:         </Space>
  108:       ),
  109:     },
  110:     {
  111:       title: 'Người thực hiện',
  112:       dataIndex: 'actorId',
  113:       key: 'actorId',
  114:       width: 140,
  115:       render: (id: number) => <Text>Account #{id}</Text>,
  116:     },
  117:     {
  118:       title: 'IP',
  119:       dataIndex: 'ipAddress',
  120:       key: 'ipAddress',
  121:       width: 140,
  122:       render: (ip?: string) => <Text code>{ip || '—'}</Text>,
  123:     },
  124:     {
  125:       title: 'Thời gian',
  126:       dataIndex: 'createdAt',
  127:       key: 'createdAt',
  128:       width: 180,
  129:       render: (v: string) => <DateDisplay value={v} format="long" />,
  130:     },
  131:     {
  132:       title: 'Chi tiết',
  133:       key: 'detail',
  134:       width: 100,
  135:       align: 'center',
  136:       fixed: 'right',
  137:       render: (_, record) => (
  138:         <Tooltip title="Xem chi tiết">
  139:           <Button
  140:             type="text"
  141:             icon={<EyeOutlined />}
  142:             onClick={() => setDetailLog(record)}
  143:           />
  144:         </Tooltip>
  145:       ),
  146:     },
  147:   ];
  148: 
  149:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  150:   // RENDER
  151:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  152:   return (
  153:     <PageContainer>
  154:       <PageHeader
  155:         title="Nhật ký hệ thống"
  156:         subtitle={`Tổng ${total} bản ghi`}
  157:         extra={
  158:           <Button
  159:             icon={<ReloadOutlined />}
  160:             onClick={() => {
  161:               fetchData();
  162:               toast.success('Đã làm mới');
  163:             }}
  164:           >
  165:             Làm mới
  166:           </Button>
  167:         }
  168:       />
  169: 
  170:       <div style={{ background: '#fff', padding: 16, borderRadius: 8 }}>
  171:         <DataTable<AuditLogResponse>
  172:           columns={columns}
  173:           dataSource={data}
  174:           loading={loading}
  175:           rowKey="id"
  176:           total={total}
  177:           page={page}
  178:           pageSize={pageSize}
  179:           onPageChange={(p, ps) => {
  180:             setPage(p);
  181:             setPageSize(ps);
  182:           }}
  183:           showSearch={false}
  184:           extra={
  185:             <Space>
  186:               <Select
  187:                 placeholder="Đối tượng"
  188:                 allowClear
  189:                 style={{ width: 160 }}
  190:                 value={entityTypeFilter}
  191:                 onChange={setEntityTypeFilter}
  192:                 options={ENTITY_TYPES.map((t) => ({ value: t, label: t }))}
  193:               />
  194:               <Select
  195:                 placeholder="Hành động"
  196:                 allowClear
  197:                 style={{ width: 160 }}
  198:                 value={actionFilter}
  199:                 onChange={setActionFilter}
  200:                 options={ACTIONS.map((a) => ({ value: a, label: a }))}
  201:               />
  202:             </Space>
  203:           }
  204:         />
  205:       </div>
  206: 
  207:       {/* Detail Modal */}
  208:       <Modal
  209:         title={`Chi tiết log #${detailLog?.id || ''}`}
  210:         open={!!detailLog}
  211:         onCancel={() => setDetailLog(null)}
  212:         footer={[
  213:           <Button key="close" type="primary" onClick={() => setDetailLog(null)}>
  214:             Đóng
  215:           </Button>,
  216:         ]}
  217:         width={700}
  218:       >
  219:         {detailLog && (
  220:           <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
  221:             <div>
  222:               <Text strong>Hành động:</Text>{' '}
  223:               <Tag color={actionColor(detailLog.action)}>{detailLog.action}</Tag>
  224:             </div>
  225:             <div>
  226:               <Text strong>Đối tượng:</Text> {detailLog.entityType}
  227:               {detailLog.entityId && ` #${detailLog.entityId}`}
  228:             </div>
  229:             <div>
  230:               <Text strong>Người thực hiện:</Text> Account #{detailLog.actorId}
  231:             </div>
  232:             <div>
  233:               <Text strong>IP:</Text> <Text code>{detailLog.ipAddress || '—'}</Text>
  234:             </div>
  235:             <div>
  236:               <Text strong>Thời gian:</Text>{' '}
  237:               <DateDisplay value={detailLog.createdAt} format="long" />
  238:             </div>
  239: 
  240:             {detailLog.oldValue && (
  241:               <div>
  242:                 <Text strong>Giá trị cũ:</Text>
  243:                 <pre style={{
  244:                   background: '#fff1f0',
  245:                   padding: 12,
  246:                   borderRadius: 4,
  247:                   fontSize: 12,
  248:                   maxHeight: 200,
  249:                   overflow: 'auto',
  250:                 }}>
  251:                   {JSON.stringify(detailLog.oldValue, null, 2)}
  252:                 </pre>
  253:               </div>
  254:             )}
  255: 
  256:             {detailLog.newValue && (
  257:               <div>
  258:                 <Text strong>Giá trị mới:</Text>
  259:                 <pre style={{
  260:                   background: '#f6ffed',
  261:                   padding: 12,
  262:                   borderRadius: 4,
  263:                   fontSize: 12,
  264:                   maxHeight: 200,
  265:                   overflow: 'auto',
  266:                 }}>
  267:                   {JSON.stringify(detailLog.newValue, null, 2)}
  268:                 </pre>
  269:               </div>
  270:             )}
  271:           </div>
  272:         )}
  273:       </Modal>
  274:     </PageContainer>
  275:   );
  276: };
  277: 
  278: export default SystemLogPage;

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/pages/admin/users/UserDetailPage.tsx
// ============================================================
    1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
    2: import { useParams, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
    3: import {
    4:   Card, Descriptions, Avatar, Button, Space, Select, Tag, Divider,
    5:   Typography, Spin, Result, List, Empty,
    6: } from 'antd';
    7: import {
    8:   UserOutlined, ArrowLeftOutlined, SaveOutlined,
    9: } from '@ant-design/icons';
   10: import toast from 'react-hot-toast';
   11: 
   12: import PageContainer from '@/components/common/PageContainer';
   13: import PageHeader from '@/components/common/PageHeader';
   14: import StatusTag from '@/components/common/StatusTag';
   15: import DateDisplay from '@/components/common/DateDisplay';
   16: 
   17: import { adminUserApi, adminRoleApi } from '@/services/api/admin';
   18: import type { AccountResponse, Role, AccountPermissionResponse } from '@/services/types/user';
   19: 
   20: const { Title, Text } = Typography;
   21: 
   22: const UserDetailPage = () => {
   23:   const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
   24:   const navigate = useNavigate();
   25:   const accountId = Number(id);
   26: 
   27:   const [user, setUser] = useState<AccountResponse | null>(null);
   28:   const [roles, setRoles] = useState<Role[]>([]);
   29:   const [permissions, setPermissions] = useState<AccountPermissionResponse[]>([]);
   30:   const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] = useState<number | undefined>();
   31:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   32:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
   33:   const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
   34: 
   35:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   36:   // FETCH
   37:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   38:   useEffect(() => {
   39:     if (!accountId || isNaN(accountId)) {
   40:       setError('ID không hợp lệ');
   41:       setLoading(false);
   42:       return;
   43:     }
   44: 
   45:     (async () => {
   46:       setLoading(true);
   47:       try {
   48:         const [userData, roleList, permList] = await Promise.all([
   49:           adminUserApi.getById(accountId),
   50:           adminRoleApi.list(),
   51:           adminUserApi.getPermissions(accountId).catch(() => []),
   52:         ]);
   53:         setUser(userData);
   54:         setRoles(roleList);
   55:         setPermissions(permList);
   56:         setSelectedRole(userData.roleId);
   57:       } catch (err) {
   58:         const e = err as { message?: string };
   59:         setError(e?.message || 'Không thể tải thông tin tài khoản');
   60:       } finally {
   61:         setLoading(false);
   62:       }
   63:     })();
   64:   }, [accountId]);
   65: 
   66:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   67:   // HANDLERS
   68:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   69:   const handleAssignRole = async () => {
   70:     if (!selectedRole || selectedRole === user?.roleId) {
   71:       toast.error('Vui lòng chọn vai trò khác');
   72:       return;
   73:     }
   74:     setSaving(true);
   75:     try {
   76:       await adminUserApi.assignRole(accountId, selectedRole);
   77:       const newRole = roles.find((r) => r.id === selectedRole);
   78:       toast.success(`Đã đổi vai trò thành ${newRole?.code}`);
   79:       // Reload
   80:       const updated = await adminUserApi.getById(accountId);
   81:       setUser(updated);
   82:     } catch (err) {
   83:       const e = err as { message?: string };
   84:       toast.error(e?.message || 'Đổi vai trò thất bại');
   85:     } finally {
   86:       setSaving(false);
   87:     }
   88:   };
   89: 
   90:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   91:   // RENDER
   92:   // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   93:   if (loading) {
   94:     return (
   95:       <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 80 }}>
   96:         <Spin size="large" />
   97:       </div>
   98:     );
   99:   }
  100: 
  101:   if (error || !user) {
  102:     return (
  103:       <Result
  104:         status="404"
  105:         title="Không tìm thấy"
  106:         subTitle={error || 'Tài khoản không tồn tại'}
  107:         extra={
  108:           <Button type="primary" onClick={() => navigate('/admin/users')}>
  109:             Về danh sách
  110:           </Button>
  111:         }
  112:       />
  113:     );
  114:   }
  115: 
  116:   return (
  117:     <PageContainer>
  118:       <PageHeader
  119:         title={`Tài khoản #${user.accountId}`}
  120:         subtitle={user.email}
  121:         backButton
  122:         backTo="/admin/users"
  123:         extra={
  124:           <Button icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate('/admin/users')}>
  125:             Quay lại
  126:           </Button>
  127:         }
  128:       />
  129: 
  130:       <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '2fr 1fr', gap: 24 }}>
  131:         {/* ── Cột trái: Thông tin ── */}
  132:         <div>
  133:           <Card>
  134:             <Space size={24} align="center" style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
  135:               <Avatar
  136:                 size={80}
  137:                 src={user.avatarUrl || undefined}
  138:                 icon={!user.avatarUrl ? <UserOutlined /> : undefined}
  139:                 style={{ backgroundColor: '#1677ff' }}
  140:               />
  141:               <div>
  142:                 <Title level={4} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
  143:                   {user.fullName || 'Chưa cập nhật'}
  144:                 </Title>
  145:                 <Text type="secondary">{user.email}</Text>
  146:                 <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
  147:                   <StatusTag status={user.roleCode || ''} />
  148:                   {user.isLocked ? (
  149:                     <Tag color="red" style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>Đã khóa</Tag>
  150:                   ) : user.isActive ? (
  151:                     <Tag color="green" style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>Hoạt động</Tag>
  152:                   ) : (
  153:                     <Tag style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}>Không hoạt động</Tag>
  154:                   )}
  155:                 </div>
  156:               </div>
  157:             </Space>
  158: 
  159:             <Divider />
  160: 
  161:             <Descriptions column={2} bordered size="small">
  162:               <Descriptions.Item label="ID">{user.accountId}</Descriptions.Item>
  163:               <Descriptions.Item label="Email">{user.email}</Descriptions.Item>
  164:               <Descriptions.Item label="Họ tên">{user.fullName || '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
  165:               <Descriptions.Item label="SĐT">{user.phone || '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
  166:               <Descriptions.Item label="Vai trò">
  167:                 <StatusTag status={user.roleCode || ''} />
  168:               </Descriptions.Item>
  169:               <Descriptions.Item label="Xác minh email">
  170:                 {user.emailVerifiedAt ? (
  171:                   <Tag color="green">Đã xác minh</Tag>
  172:                 ) : (
  173:                   <Tag>Chưa xác minh</Tag>
  174:                 )}
  175:               </Descriptions.Item>
  176:               <Descriptions.Item label="Đăng nhập cuối">
  177:                 <DateDisplay value={user.lastLoginAt} format="long" />
  178:               </Descriptions.Item>
  179:               <Descriptions.Item label="Ngày tạo">
  180:                 <DateDisplay value={user.createdAt} format="long" />
  181:               </Descriptions.Item>
  182:               <Descriptions.Item label="Cập nhật cuối" span={2}>
  183:                 <DateDisplay value={user.updatedAt} format="long" />
  184:               </Descriptions.Item>
  185:             </Descriptions>
  186:           </Card>
  187: 
  188:           {/* Permissions */}
  189:           <Card title="Quyền được gán" style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>
  190:             {permissions.length === 0 ? (
  191:               <Empty description="Chưa có quyền nào được gán" />
  192:             ) : (
  193:               <List
  194:                 dataSource={permissions}
  195:                 renderItem={(item) => (
  196:                   <List.Item>
  197:                     <List.Item.Meta
  198:                       title={
  199:                         <Space>
  200:                           <Tag color="blue">{item.method}</Tag>
  201:                           <Text code>{item.endpointPattern}</Text>
  202:                         </Space>
  203:                       }
  204:                       description={item.description || item.module}
  205:                     />
  206:                     {item.isEnabled ? (
  207:                       <Tag color="green">Active</Tag>
  208:                     ) : (
  209:                       <Tag>Disabled</Tag>
  210:                     )}
  211:                   </List.Item>
  212:                 )}
  213:               />
  214:             )}
  215:           </Card>
  216:         </div>
  217: 
  218:         {/* ── Cột phải: Đổi role ── */}
  219:         <div>
  220:           <Card title="Đổi vai trò">
  221:             <Space direction="vertical" style={{ width: '100%' }} size={16}>
  222:               <div>
  223:                 <Text type="secondary">Vai trò hiện tại</Text>
  224:                 <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
  225:                   <StatusTag status={user.roleCode || ''} />
  226:                 </div>
  227:               </div>
  228: 
  229:               <div>
  230:                 <Text type="secondary">Đổi sang</Text>
  231:                 <Select
  232:                   style={{ width: '100%', marginTop: 8 }}
  233:                   placeholder="Chọn vai trò"
  234:                   value={selectedRole}
  235:                   onChange={setSelectedRole}
  236:                   options={roles.map((r) => ({
  237:                     value: r.id,
  238:                     label: `${r.code} - ${r.description || ''}`,
  239:                   }))}
  240:                 />
  241:               </div>
  242: 
  243:               <Button
  244:                 type="primary"
  245:                 block
  246:                 icon={<SaveOutlined />}
  247:                 onClick={handleAssignRole}
  248:                 loading={saving}
  249:                 disabled={!selectedRole || selectedRole === user.roleId}
  250:               >
  251:                 Lưu vai trò
  252:               </Button>
  253: 
  254:               <Divider style={{ margin: '8px 0' }} />
  255: 
  256:               <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
  257:                 ⚠️ Sau khi đổi vai trò, user cần đăng nhập lại để áp dụng
  258:                 quyền mới.
  259:               </Text>
  260:             </Space>
  261:           </Card>
  262:         </div>
  263:       </div>
  264:     </PageContainer>
  265:   );
  266: };
